## Document 006: 1300亿美元财富一日蒸发，预测市场80%胜率翻车，巴西大选为美国中期选举敲响警钟

Source PDF: all report pdf/1300亿美元财富一日蒸发，预测市场80%胜率翻车，巴西大选为美国中期选举敲响警钟.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-28
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

巴西⼤选预测市场严重失准， Polymarket 和 Kalshi 赛前将 Bolsonaro 胜率标在约 80% ，卢
拉却意外胜出，导致约 3200 万美元合约成交量催⽣的约 1300 亿美元股市财富蒸发。事件暴
露预测市场流动性不⾜、参与者结构偏差与信息质量缺陷，为美国中期选举敲响警钟：不
可轻信赔率直接定价⾦融资产。
摘要：
预测市场在巴西⼤选中遭遇重⼤失准，引发了市场对这⼀新兴⼯具可靠性的深刻质疑。
巴西总统⼤选决选于 10 ⽉ 25 ⽇落幕，现任左翼总统路易斯 · 伊纳西乌 · 卢拉 · 达席尔⽡（ Luiz
Inácio Lula da Silva ）出⼈意料地击败保守派候选⼈ Flávio Bolsonaro 。在投票⽇之前，
Polymarket 和 Kalshi 两⼤预测市场均将 Bolsonaro 的胜选概率标定在约 80% 。巴西雷亚尔与基
准股指 Ibovespa 在选前⼀路⾛⾼，反映出市场对 Bolsonaro 胜选的强烈预期。然⽽随着卢拉胜选
消息传出，市场随即⼤幅逆转，做多投资者蒙受了沉重损失。

这⼀事件所引发的影响远不⽌于巴西本⾝。区区约 3200 万美元的 Polymarket 合约成交量，在选
前数周内催⽣了约 1300 亿美元的股市财富，⽽这些财富在市场重新开盘后随即灰⻜烟灭。彭博
专栏作家 Jonathan Levin 指出，此次事件更值得警惕的教训，在于传统投资者轻信预测市场赔
率、将其直接⽤于⾦融资产定价的做法，⽽这⼀⻛险在即将到来的美国中期选举前⼣尤为突
出。
预测市场的先天缺陷
预测市场允许⽤⼾就现实世界事件的结果相互对赌，近⼀年来已饱受争议。据报道，美国司法
部曾指控⼀名美国⼠兵利⽤有关突袭捕获时任委内瑞拉总统尼古拉斯 · ⻢杜罗的机密信息，在
Polymarket 上下注获利；检察官亦指控 Alphabet Inc. ⼀名软件⼯程师利⽤内部数据，在⾕歌 " 年
度搜索 " 结果上进⾏押注。批评⼈⼠还担忧，预测市场带有成瘾性赌博属性，并可能在体育和政
治领域制造腐败诱因，同时形成危险的反馈循环 —— 赌盘赔率左右传统媒体和社交媒体的内容
基调，进⽽影响公众认知乃⾄⺠主选举结果。

⽀持者则认为，预测市场在⼀定程度上能够汇聚群体智慧，为⻛险管理和公众决策提供参考，
并模拟出过去只有对冲基⾦、保险公司等机构才能获取的预测模型输出。然⽽当合约规模较
⼩、流动性不⾜时，预测市场所传递的信号可能误导多于启发，且市场的信息质量完全取决于
参与者的认知⽔平。
三重警⽰：流动性、参与者与信息质量
此次巴西事件中，上述隐患早已有迹可循。巴西⼤选的 Polymarket 合约总成交量约为 2.49 亿美
元，与 2024 年美国总统⼤选 37 亿美元的规模相去甚远， Kalshi 市场的体量则更⼩。
更关键的是，在 10 ⽉ 4 ⽇第⼀轮投票⾄ 10 ⽉ 25 ⽇决选期间， Bolsonaro 的胜选概率从约 64% 跳升
⾄ 80% ，但这⼀阶段 Polymarket 的成交量不⾜ 3200 万美元。其中⼤量交易集中在第⼀轮投票前
后，以及决选⽇尾盘 —— 彼时最⾼法院⼤法官 Cristiano Zanin 单⽅⾯宣布将投票时间延⻓两⼩
时，引发市场剧烈波动。值得注意的是， Zanin 曾担任卢拉的私⼈律师。

参与者结构同样存在严重问题。巴西财政部于今年 4 ⽉封锁了 Polymarket 和 Kalshi 的访问权限，
政府 9 ⽉⼜颁布了更⼴泛的⽹络赌博禁令。这意味着，在决选关键窗⼝期内推动赔率变动的，⼏
乎全为⾮巴西本地⽤⼾，其中不乏来⾃美国的加密货币圈⼈⼠。据报道，埃隆 · ⻢斯克于 9 ⽉ 20
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

⽇转发了⼀篇已删除的 X 平台帖⼦，内容涉及 Polymarket 赔率看好 Bolsonaro ，或进⼀步助推了
相关资⾦流⼊。⻢斯克⻓期以来对 Bolsonaro 家族及拉丁美洲右翼势⼒持⽀持⽴场。
⺠调早有预警，市场视⽽不⻅
与 预 测 市 场 的 ⾼ 置 信 度 形 成 鲜 明 对 ⽐ 的 是 ， 传 统 ⺠ 调 从 未 显 ⽰ 出 如 此 悬 殊 的 差 距 。 BTG
Pactual/Nexus 在决选前⼀天发布的⺠调显⽰， Bolsonaro ⽀持率为 50.5% ，卢拉为 49.5% ，差距
在 2 个百分点的误差范围之内。 Datafolha 于 10 ⽉ 22 ⽇公布的调查则显⽰两⼈⽀持率完全持平。
尽管巴西观察⼈⼠习惯于预期 Bolsonaro 家族的实际得票会⾼于⺠调，但从上述数据到 80% 的胜

选概率，这⼀逻辑跳跃难以⾃圆其说。 Jonathan Levin 指出，令⼈惊讶的并⾮预测市场本⾝出现
误差，⽽是众多投资者在明显的预警信号⾯前，仍将这⼀赔率直接转化为⾦融资产的定价依
据。
对美国中期选举的警⽰
巴西事件对即将举⾏的美国中期选举具有直接的借鉴意义，但两者之间也存在重要差异 ——最
关键的⼀点在于，美国市场有⼤量真实选⺠参与。
即便如此，投资者和选⺠仍需汲取以下教训：其⼀，中期选举的预测市场交易活跃度通常远低
于总统⼤选，许多具体选区合约的流动性远不及巴西⼤选；其⼆，不应将任何结果视为板上钉
钉 —— 这种先⼊之⻅或许正是让部分 Bolsonaro ⽀持者在决选⽇选择放弃投票、最终帮助卢拉翻
盘的原因之⼀；其三，若要参考预测市场赔率，必须对相关市场的质量和参与者结构进⾏独⽴
评估。
当预测市场与传统⾦融市场相互叠加时，误判的代价将被成倍放⼤。巴西的案例已然清晰地呈
现了这⼀⻛险的量级。若同样的情形在美国中期选举中重演，其冲击将远不⽌于此。

海外经济分化加深下中国外贸动能进一步增强 
——2026 年 9 月进出口数据点评

事件： 2026 年9 月，我国进出口总额为7608.4 亿美元，同比
增长 34.1%。其中，出口 4471.8 亿美元，同比增长 36.3%；进
口3136.5 亿美元，同比增长31.0%；贸易顺差1335.3 亿美元，
接近历史最高点，同比增长50.5%。 
一、9 月进出口数据解析 
◼ 9 月出口增速明显加快，外需改善与科技产业链韧性共同推动
出口高增。8 月我国出口同比增长 25.0%，较 7 月加快 1.1 个
百分点，明显高于2021—2025 年8 月约7.1%的平均增速。9 月
我国出口总体高增的因素有二：一是海外制造业保持扩张，为
出口增长提供需求基础。9 月全球制造业 PMI 由 8 月的 52.3%
升至 53.0%，美国、欧元区、日本和韩国制造业 PMI 均处于扩
张区间，表明海外生产活动仍有韧性，对中间品和制成品的需
求形成支撑；二是前期台风、港口延误及航线调整使部分货物
延后至 9 月出运，叠加海外旺季备货和国庆长假前集中报关交
付，进一步放大了当月增幅。总体看，9 月出口高增既反映内
外需求和生产活动改善，也包含交付节奏变化带来的短期影响。 
➢ 亚洲市场仍是主要拉动力量，对欧美出口同步提速，出口市
场结构改善。9 月我国对东盟出口同比增长 52.3%，较 8 月
加快 22.1 个百分点，出口金额达到 817.0 亿美元，占出口
总额的 18.3%，对整体出口增速拉动约 8.5 个百分点，继续
稳居第一大出口市场。对韩国出口增长 68.2%，对印度、俄
罗斯出口分别增长 47.8%和 51.1%，显示亚洲生产网络和区
域投资需求仍然活跃。与此同时，对欧美出口明显改善：

www.ccxi.com.cn
海外经济分化加深下外贸韧性能维持多久？
——2026 年8 月进出口数据点评
对美国出口增长39.6%，较8 月加快5.2 个百分点；对欧盟出口增长25.3%，较8 月
加快18.7 个百分点，二者合计拉动出口增长约7.8 个百分点。其他市场中，对拉丁
美洲出口增速由 17.5%升至 24.7%，对非洲出口虽由 30.9%回落至 16.1%，但仍保持
两位数增长。
➢ 算力相关产品仍是出口增长核心，汽车和传统商品贡献同步上升。  9 月机电产品出
口同比增长 43.7%，占出口总额的 66.7%，对整体出口增速拉动 27.7 个百分点，较
8 月提高 7.4 个百分点。其中，集成电路和自动数据处理设备及其零部件分别增长
136.0%和 92.9%，合计拉动出口增长约 13.0 个百分点，全球 AI 基础设施建设及半
导体产业景气仍是出口的主要支撑；汽车及底盘出口增长 62.0%，拉动作用由 8 月
的 1.7 个百分点升至 2.4 个百分点。与此同时，受欧美市场为年底购物季集中补库

拉动， 传统商品出口呈现普遍改善，服装、纺织品、玩具和家用电器分别增长
23.8%、19.0%、40.8%和 31.7%，塑料制品和家具出口也明显提速，上述六类商品合
计拉动出口增长约 3.8 个百分点。总体看，9 月出口呈现“高技术产品继续领跑、
汽车动能增强、传统商品全面回升”的特征，增长基础较前期进一步拓宽。

——2026 年8 月进出口数据点评 
◼ 进口增速继续加快，生产活动回升和出口产业链备货共同支撑进口需求。 9 月进口同
比增长31.0%，较8 月加快2.8 个百分点，进口金额环比增长11.1%，明显强于历史同
期表现。一方面，制造业生产指数明显回升，出口增速同步加快，带动企业增加中间
品、零部件及能源原材料采购；另一方面，算力基础设施和高端制造投资持续推进，
对相关设备和关键零部件形成较强进口需求。总体看，9 月进口高增主要反映生产性
需求和出口产业链备货增强，传统内需的改善仍相对有限。 
➢ 从进口产品结构来看，高技术产品仍是主要支撑，能源和资源品贡献有所改善但
内部分化明显。9 月高新技术产品进口同比增长 63.9%，占进口总额的 41.6%，拉
动整体进口增长 21.2 个百分点。其中，自动数据处理设备及其零部件进口增长

296.5%，拉动作用由 8 月的 6.1 个百分点升至 8.9 个百分点；集成电路进口增长
74.7%，拉动进口增长 12.8 个百分点，反映算力建设及相关制造业生产对芯片、
服务器零部件等中间品的需求进一步释放。资源品方面，原油进口由上月负向拉
动转为小幅正向贡献，天然气和煤炭分别拉动进口增长 0.7 和 0.6 个百分点；但
铁矿石同比拉动转负，农产品贡献也相对有限。总体看， 9 月进口结构延 续“高
技术产品贡献集中、能源商品边际改善、其他资源品表现分化”的特征。 
◼ 三季度我国进出口总额为 21278.0 亿美元，同比增长 28.7%；其中，出口 12464.6 亿美
元，同比增长 28.5%，进口 8813.4 亿美元，同比增长 29.0%，贸易顺差 3651.2 亿美元，
同比增长27.3%。从季度内部看，7—8 月出口保持20%以上增长，9 月进一步升至36.3%；
图 7：9 月我国进口增速达 31% 图 8：9 月我国进口增速表现强于季节性

5 
www.ccxi.com.cn 
海外经济分化加深下外贸韧性能维持多久？ 
——2026 年8 月进出口数据点评 
进口则连续三个月保持较快增长，进出口增长的同步性进一步增强。前三季度我国进出
口总额同比增长 23.8%，其中出口增长 21.3%、进口增长 27.5%，累计贸易顺差同比增长
7.8%。与上半年相比，三季度外贸不仅增速进一步提升，增长结构也由 高技术产品和亚
洲市场主导，逐步转向市场与商品结构多元化。 
◼ 展望后续，全球制造业周期上行、新兴产业需求和贸易关系局部缓和仍将支撑我国出
口，四季度出口增速仍将保持高位，全年出口累计增速有望超 20%，但仍需关注外需持
续性、贸易规则变化以及能源运输成本上升带来的压力。 支撑端来看，全球 AI 基础设
施投资扩张周期尚未结束，仍将对我国机电、高技术产品出口形成持续拉动；9 月下旬
中美就约 300 亿美元非敏感商品达成互降关税框架协议，并开启人工智能领域对话，有
助于缓和双边贸易进一步恶化的风险。10 月上旬，中欧也就我国混动及插电混动汽车对
欧出口开展调节磋商，双边贸易摩擦边际缓和。但也要看到，主要经济体的贸易限制正
由加征关税向配额管理、原产地审查、出口管制和产业规则延伸，政策环境并未实质性
转松。海外高利率、能源和运输成本高企，也可能削弱终端需求并压缩出口利润。后续
出口能否保持较快增长，既取决于科技产业链景气的持续性，也取决于传统商品需求改
善和贸易环境缓和能否形成更多增量。

二、全球经济跟踪：能源冲击推动全球主要经济体货币政策再收紧，增长分化与融资压力同
步加大 
9 月中旬以来，全球经济呈现增长保持韧性与通胀压力回升并存的特征。中东局势推动
油价和运输成本高位波动，美欧日央行相继加息，带动全球长端利率和美元走强，新兴市场
资本流出压力初步显现；与此同时，AI 投资和供应链适应能力仍对货物贸易和部分权益市场
形成支撑，但就业、居民购买力以及运输和旅游等传统行业面临的压力有所加大。总体来看，
全球经济短期内陷入同步衰退的可能性不高，但增长动力进一步向科技相关领域集中，高利
率与能源风险仍将制约需求修复。 
➢ 从经济增长、通胀与货币政策来看，本轮加息并非源于全球需求普遍过热，主要
是为了防止供给冲击转化为持续通胀。 9 月 16 日，美联储加息 25 个基点至
3.75%—4.00%，并将2026 年PCE 通胀预测上调至3.7%、年末政策利率预测中值上
调至 4.1%，政策重心重新偏向控制通胀。当前美国科技投资和居民消费仍有一定
韧性，但就业已边际降温，对后续加息节奏和空间形成影响。美国 9 月新增非农

### Page 7

6
www.ccxi.com.cn
海外经济分化加深下外贸韧性能维持多久？
——2026 年8 月进出口数据点评
就业仅2.9 万人，失业率升至4.2%，高利率以及AI 替代对就业和传统行业的抑制
有所显现。欧洲 9 月 HICP 同比升至 3.8%，其中能源价格同比上涨 18.8%，推动欧
洲央行将存 款便利利率上调至 2.50%；日本央行也在日元偏弱和进口成本压力下
将政策利率上调至约 1.25%。整体来看，主要央行短期内均缺乏转向宽松的条件，
但随着就业放缓、购买力承压，持续加息可能加大需求降温压力，后续政策可能
在抑制通胀与维持增长之间更加审慎。
➢ 从主要资产表现上来看，全球利率中枢进一步上移，市场对高利率持续时间的预
期明显延长。9 月中旬以来，美国10 年期国债收益率由约5.00%升至当前的5.21%，
英国 10 年期国债收益率升至约 5.40%，日本 10 年期国债收益率则在 3%附近高位
波动，反映能源通胀、财政供给和央行加息预期仍在推高期限溢价。同期美元指
数由约 99.6 升至 102.2，进一步收紧了非美经济体的外部融资条件。据 IIF 统计，

9 月新兴市场股票和债券资金合计净流出263 亿美元，为6 月以来首次出现月度净
流出。权益市场方面，受到科技产业景气和企业盈利预期支撑，9 月中旬至 10 月
13 日，标普500 指数和日经225 指数分别上涨约2.9%和6.8%，而A 股与港股仍受
内需修复力度、外部金融条件收紧以及盈利改善预期制约，上证综指和恒生指数
分别回落约1.6%和1.3%。
➢ 贸易和供应链方面，全球货物贸易呈现韧性，但增长动力较为集中，能源冲击对
运输、旅游等服务贸易的拖累仍然突出。10 月 8 日，WTO 将 2026 年全球货物贸易
量增速预测由 3 月的 1.9%大幅上调至 3.9%，并将 2027 年预测上调至 4.1%。上调
预测主要源于全球供应链对能源和运输冲击的适应能力好于预期，以及 AI 基础设
施投资带动半导体、服务器等相关产品贸易快速增长。但这种韧性并不代表全球
贸易已经全面回暖，受高燃油成本、航线调整和国际旅行需求受抑制影响， WTO
将2026 年服务贸易增速预测由4.8%下调至3.3%，运输和旅游成为主要拖累。9 月

中旬以来，布伦特原油价格在约 120—140 美元/桶之间宽幅震荡，当前仍处于约
131 美元/桶的高位，同期原油运输指数由约 4500 点升至接近 8000 点，说明能源
供应和运输风险尚未明显缓解。总体来看，AI 投资和供应链韧性支撑了全球货物
贸易，但高油价正在通过运输成本、航空燃油和居民购买力等渠道压制服务贸易，
全球贸易呈现科技产品偏强、能源密集型行业和传统服务业偏弱的结构性分化。

京元電子 (2449 TT) 
狼來了，狼還沒來

永豐觀點 
下修 4Q26 營運展望，以反映持續的來料不順。 
 投資評價與建議 
目標價維持 320 元：京元電短期逆風未平，然 2027 年將迎來強勁成長，係考量
(1) 年底為 CPU、網通客戶等增加的產能，可自 1Q27 起完整貢獻營收，(2) 京元
電有機會增加在主要客戶 TPU 的市占率，目前已在和客戶討論下世代晶片搭配的
預燒爐，(3) HPC 新品放量，採用液冷預燒爐帶動測試單價提升。惟須留意產能快
速擴張下的成本增加。評價面，以2027 年修正後 EPS 推算目前 PER 約 23.3x，考
量 AI/HPC 相關需求未見軟弱，建議拉回再行佈局，目標價 320 元(25.2x 2027 
EPS(F))。

ESG 評析 
京元電企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向
平均得分分別為 B+等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2023 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

京元電為國內 IC 測試大廠：成立於 1987 年，主要提供前段晶圓測試(CP)及後段
IC 成品測試(FT)、預燒等服務，近年透過自製測試設備增加取得 AI 客戶信任，同
時降低封裝業務來改善公司平均獲利能力。 2Q26 營收產品組合為晶圓測試
29.6%、產品測試 61%、產品預燒 7.7%、封裝 1.3%、其他 0.4%。應用別營收比
重為消費性電子 30.3%、通訊 17.5%、數據處理與儲存 36.3%、汽車電子 13.0%、
工業 2.5%、其他 0.4%。目前主要生產據點位於桃園楊梅、苗栗竹南、苗栗頭份、
苗栗銅鑼廠區，並正在新加坡、美國等地進行佈局。主要客戶包含全球主要半導體
公司，如聯發科、韋爾半導體、AMD(Xilinx)、意法半導體等，主要競爭對手為日
月光、矽格等。 
美國廠放量或在 2028 年後 ：研究部預期台積電美國先進封裝廠區將於 2H28-2029
年進入量產，京元電尚未公布美國廠的具體時程與選址，但我們認為 AeternZ 廠
址存在落腳德州的可能性，理由有三：(1) 下游組裝廠已在當地佈局，(2) 亞利桑
那州須與晶圓廠爭搶技術人力，(3) 京元電位處後段測試，靠近終端組裝較符合供
應鏈配置。另外，本次公告的客戶預付款，我們認為宣示意義大於實質挹注，係因
封測廠資本支出以設備為大宗 ， 而該客戶的測試設備多已採託管(consignment)模
式，京元電本身需投入的設備資金不高，預付款對降低資本支出壓力的效果有限。
此外 ，京元電赴美設廠短期內本就以服務少數關鍵客戶為主 ， 此公告屬預期內的進
展，我們不建議過度解讀。 
來 料 不 如 預 期 ， 下 修 短 期 展 望 ：研 究 部 下修 京 元 電4Q26 營收至 131 億
(+8.4%QoQ，+32.0%YoY， 原估143 億)， 主要考量(1) 上游來料不順 ， 造成AI/HPC
相關產品放量幅度不如預期，而對應的 CPU 出貨則穩定墊高，(2) 客戶下修非旗
艦機款封測需求。獲利部分同樣因營收放大不如預期、新產能開出，抵銷掉少了夏
季電費的正向影響，預估毛利率 39.2%，稅後淨利 29.8 億(+14.9%QoQ，
+32.8%YoY)，EPS 2.32 元。 
2027 年展新機：目前我們並未見到 AI/HPC 終端需求轉弱的跡象，因此京元電業
績拉升僅是時間問題，預期 2027 年受惠(1) 年底為 CPU、網通等客戶增加的產
能，可自 1Q27 起完整貢獻營收，(2) 京元電有機會增加在主要客戶 TPU 的市站
率，目前已在和客戶討論下世代晶片搭配的預燒爐，(3) HPC 新品放量，採用液冷
預燒爐帶動測試單價提升。從供給端來看，楊梅廠、頭份一廠、頭份二廠的無塵室
在 1H27 陸續開出後，我們認為2027 年京元電的成長將不再受到產能限制，而是
看客戶的拉貨節奏。 
財務預估：研究部預估京元電 2026 年營收 466 億(+33.5%YoY)，毛利率 39.1%，
稅後淨利 103.5 億(-6.0%YoY，年減主因 2025 年公司處分蘇州廠房)，EPS 8.07
元(原估 8.81 元)。2027 年營收 682 億(+46.3%YoY)，毛利率 41.1%，稅後淨利
163.2 億(+57.6%YoY)，EPS 12.72 元(原估 13.93 元)。

Quanta (2382.TW): AI servers ramp up to drive growth ahead; model 
transition and PC shipment slowdown could weigh on 4Q26; Neutral
19 October 2026 | 1:57AM HKT
Quanta Sep revenues declined by 23% MoM, which we attributed to AI servers model 
transition, while PC entering quarter-end at strong MoM of +62% MoM. Looking 
forward, we expect monthly revenues to resume sequential growth in Oct to Dec, 
riding on reducing impact of AI servers model transition as new racks ramping up, 
along with growing general servers on agentic AI, and ASP increase on better 
specification and high raw material costs. We expect 4Q26E revenues to decline by 
4% QoQ, considering the high base in 3Q26 and the gradual ramp up of new AI 
server racks. As we highlighted in Oct (Global Server TAM updates), we expect global 
AI server racks (powered by NVIDIA and AMD) shipment at 63k / 110k / 164k in 
2026E-28E along with the specification upgrade to drive the ASP; we raised leading 
US CSPs in aggregate capex by 4% / 17% / 22% in 2026E-28E to US$1,397bn by 
2028E, reflecting our positive view on AI spending. Maintain Neutral on fair 
valuation.

Earnings revision: We raise revenues by 1% / 23% / 30% in 2026E-28E to reflect the 
AI server racks shipment ramp up and higher ASP on better specification. Given the

much lower GM of AI server racks compared to Quanta’s other products, our changes in 
gross profits narrow to 2% / 5% / 5% in 2026E-28E. Our opex ratio remains largely 
unchanged in 2026E-27E, while further improves in 2028E reflecting the enhancing 
operation efficiency when revenues scale increases and harvest from previous R&D 
investments. We change our non-op income and tax rate estimates in 2026E-28E closer 
to historical trend. Our net income is thus changed by -2% / +0% / +14% in 2026E-28E.

Valuation: We continue to use a near-term P/E to derive our 12M TP . Our target P/E 
multiple is at 10.7x 2027E (no change), which is derived from the refreshed peers’ 
correlation between forward trading P/E and earnings growth and Quanta’s forward year 
earnings growth. With our updated earnings estimates our 12M TP is revised up to 
NT$300 (vs. NT$299 previously). Our target P/E is still between the company’s historical 
avg. and avg. -1stv. trading P/E. Maintain Neutral.

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Quanta 
We are Neutral rated on Quanta. Our 12-month target price of NT$300 is based on an 
10.7x 2027E P/E, which references PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple 
correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. Key 
upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery; 2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 16 Oct 2026 close.

Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
Implications to our coverage: If next-gen AI server racks do not adopt PTFE-based
CCL, we think the outcome would skew positive for EMC and TUC but slightly

negative for Shengyi. We expect the material choice to be confirmed by mid-2027 at
the latest. Additionally, with EMC expecting its substrate CCL capacity to more than
double by end-2028, Unimicron and other substrate makers gain an alternative
supply source, which we view as positive. Substrate CCL carries higher gross
margins than PCB CCL, so a higher contribution from this business should improve
EMC’s profitability as S572T ramps from 4Q26. On ABF substrate, lead times of at

least two years for key equipment such as EPM’s ABF laminators imply that new
greenfield ABF substrate capacity can only ramp from 2029 onwards. This aligns
with our view that ABF substrate undersupply will deepen in 2027-28. Substrate
sizes going to 230mm x 230mm by 2028, implying only 4 substrates per panel, is
also supportive of our positive view on demand. This would make it very difficult to
control yield, as one area of defect on the panel would imply 25% yield loss.

PTFE CCL: Adoption of glass cloth-free PTFE CCLs in next-gen AI server systems
was the topic investors asked about most at the booths. As we have written
previously (here and here), pure PTFE CCLs could deliver the best signal integrity
(SI), making them the preferred solution for midplane/switch PCBs for some
customers. However, their processability at PCB makers remains a challenge for
now, particularly for high-layer-count boards. In addition, multilayer boards (MLBs)

using PTFE require special high-temperature presses capable of reaching close to
400°C, as standard vacuum presses are insufficient. In response, Taiwanese CCL
suppliers are also exploring other materials that could deliver M10-and-beyond
performance, including hydrocarbon (HC)-based, HC+PTFE hybrid and
benzocyclobutene (BCB)-based CCLs. We expect the CCL material for the next-gen
AI server rack to be confirmed by mid-2027 at the latest.
Other key takeaways can be found on the following page.

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
PTFE offers the best SI but is harder to process; TW CCL makers are less bullish
on PTFE and are also exploring HC, HC+PTFE hybrid & BCB.
Thin E-glass cloth shortages and price hikes to last through 1H27, at least, as
looms stay scarce and lines shift to LDK/T-glass.
EPM’s ABF laminators (95%+ global share) are a key bottleneck to ABF substrate

expansion, with 2+ year lead times and no new greenfield capacity until 2029.
Glass core substrate is likely not commercially viable in the next 2-3 years, with
crack inspection after ABF lamination currently being problematic.
October 26, 2026 01:42 PM GMT

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
Substrate CCL: S572T, the most advanced substrate CCL on display at EMC’s booth,
enters mass production in 4Q26, with volumes expected to increase sequentially
throughout 2027. EMC expects its substrate CCL capacity to more than double from
current levels by end-2028 and said it will have multiple major ABF substrate
customers. It added that it has qualified additional low-CTE glass sources to ensure
sufficient supply.
Glass cloth shortage: Several CCL suppliers still cite thin E-glass cloth as the key

material bottleneck. They generally expect the shortage to persist through 1H27
with continued price hikes, and one supplier believes it could last through late 2027.
In their view, the tightness in electronic-grade glass cloth stems from insufficient
supply of Japanese-made looms, as well as from existing glass cloth suppliers either
prioritizing in-house demand or converting production lines to LDK1/2 and T-glass
cloth to meet strong AI demand. One glass cloth supplier noted it remains reluctant

to adopt non-Toyota looms given the higher defect rates and repair costs.
ABF laminator: We also visited the booth of Eternal Materials (1717.TW, Not
Covered), whose subsidiary Eternal Precision Mechanics (EPM, 7795.TW, Not
Covered) holds 95%+ global market share in vacuum laminators used to laminate
ABF onto substrate cores. EPM’s ABF laminators are now one of the key
bottlenecks in ABF substrate capacity expansion. Our checks with EPM suggest
equipment lead times have stretched to at least two years and EPM’s new

greenfield capacity will not come online until 2029; until then, EPM can only
debottleneck by optimizing existing capacity and leasing idle capacity from third
parties. For context, a new ABF substrate plant may require at least 10 sets of EPM’s
most advanced three-stage vacuum laminator and pre-track machine, depending on
factory layout.
Glass core substrate: Microcracks and the resulting package reliability issues are
currently the two key technical bottlenecks for glass core substrates. Existing

automated optical inspection (AOI) tools struggle to detect and identify microcracks
in the glass core, and laminating multiple layers of Ajinomoto Build-up Film (ABF)
onto it makes inspection impossible, according to Shinko. One equipment supplier
also flagged metallization of high-aspect-ratio through-glass vias (TGVs) as very
challenging. We understand this supplier is not involved in copper pillar mass
transfer, an alternative approach to TGV metallization.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

營收走勢圖

研究員/張均壕 JUN_HAO1@uni-psg.com

研究部對拓凱未來營運看法轉趨正向 ，主要是考量：(1)占營收比
重最大的自行車業務在 2H26 出現改善，隨著主力客戶新品銷售表
現較佳，帶動訂單增加，預期 2027 年自行車業務有望止跌回溫。
(2)航太、安全帽及其他類業務接單保持強勁，其中航太受惠於航
空座椅需求增加，安全帽則有高階產品及新客戶訂單貢獻，加上精
密塑件業務持續擴張 ， 將有助於優化產品組合 。(3) 2Q26 受子公司
一次性費用及匯率等因素干擾，毛利率與獲利下滑，但隨下半年出
貨規模擴大、產品組合改善，預期 2H26 獲利能力將逐步回升。研
究部預估拓凱 2026 年 EPS 13.52 元(-9%YOY)，2027 年EPS 15.28
元(+13%YOY)。因公司營運重回成長軌道，且產品組合改善有利於
中長期獲利表現，故以 2027 年獲利為評價基礎，給予 12 倍本益
比，目標價 183 元，投資評等由中立調升至買進。

重點摘要：

1. 主力客戶新車種上市，自行車業務回溫 ： 
拓凱營收約有 50% 來自碳纖維自行車車架與組件，最大核心
客戶為全球知名自行車 品牌-Specialized。雖然公司的自行
車業務在1H26衰退近二成，但受惠主力客戶 年中新品上市並
在終端市場銷售亮眼 ， 客戶對拓凱積極追加車架 、 輪圈與前叉
等複材組件訂單 ， 帶動拓凱近期自行車業務顯著改善 。 在去年
下半年低基期與今年新車拉貨潮帶動下，預計 2026全年自行
車營收減幅將收斂至個位數（研究部估減少8%YoY）。2027年
隨客戶新品需求與庫存去化完畢，預 估自行車業務營收將能
回溫，成長3%YoY。

2. 安全帽、航太及其他類業務展望正向，成長動能強勁： 
自行車以外業務為拓凱營運成長的核心動能 。 其中 ， 拓凱的安
全帽業務近年出貨增加 ， 且產品結構大幅改善 ， 高階產品占比
已達2/3，預計帶動今年安全帽營收大幅增長30%YoY，且因引
進電子智能安全帽新客戶，預期明年該業務營收仍可成長
7%YoY。球拍業務方面，今年出貨暢旺，預期今年球拍營收能
創下歷史新高 ， 全年度球拍營收預計成長18%YoY。 不過 ， 因近
兩年基期拉高，明年看法較謹慎，預計小幅衰退5%YoY。航太
醫療業務方面 ， 航太接單展望樂觀 ， 預期今年該類別營收增長
14%YoY，且1Q27起新增飛機座椅訂單將正式投產 ， 預計明年該
類別營收將再有12%YoY的增速 。 另外 ， 歸類在其他類之子公司

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

新鴻洲部分，受惠自行車煞把手、電子TOF精密塑件等產品出
貨增加 ， 營運動能依舊偏強 ， 預計該業務2026年、2027年營收
分別成長23%、13%YoY。

3. 一次性負面因素淡化，3Q26毛利率與獲利將回升： 
回顧拓凱2Q26財報 ， 受提列子公司一次性費用的衝擊 ， 導致單
季毛利率下滑至31.3%(季減3個百分點，年減6.7個百分點)，
加上業外有逾6千萬元的匯損，拖累季度獲利表現，2Q26稅後
淨利2.67億元(-19%QoQ，-5%YoY)，2Q26EPS下滑至2.94元。然
而 ， 此費用屬於非經常性的短期會計調整 ， 本業高階複材競爭
力並未受損 。 下半年隨自行車業務改善 ， 拓凱3Q26合併營收回
升至27.16億元(+6%QoQ，+12%YoY)。 此外 ， 由於一次性因素淡
化 ， 加上高階安全帽 、 航太及其他類產品出貨增加 ， 產品組合
優化，研究部預估拓凱3Q26毛利率將回升至33.8%，營業利益
5.41億元(+37%QoQ，+13%YoY)，稅後淨利3.55億元(+33%QoQ，
+1%YoY)，3Q26EPS 3.91元，獲利將較第2季明顯改善。

4. 2026年營收近百億元，預估2026年EPS 13.52元： 
受惠於安全帽 、 球拍 、 航太及其他業務成長 ， 抵銷自行車業務
的衰退 ， 預期拓凱全年營收可望接近百億元 ， 研究部預估拓凱
2026年合併營收99.73億元(+8%YoY)。不過，由於2Q26受一次
性費用及匯率等因素干擾，今年度毛利率預估降至33.1%，營
業利益16.91億元(-2%YoY)，稅後淨利12.28億元(-9%YoY)，
EPS 13.52元。

5. 各業務成長帶動獲利回升，預估2027年EPS 15.28元： 
展望2027年 ， 隨自行車業務恢復成長 ， 航太及安全帽新訂單持
續貢獻，以及其他類業務穩健擴張，研究部預估 2027年營收
104.34億元(+5%YoY)， 毛利率回升至33.8%， 營業利益18.50億
元(+9%YoY)，稅後淨利13.88億元(+13%YoY)，EPS 15.28元。

6. 越南產能持續擴充，並積極佈局新應用商機 ： 
因應客戶分散生產基地與供應鏈調整需求，拓凱持續擴充越
南複合材料產品線 ， 目前除自行車前叉 、 車架等零組件外 ， 亦
逐步增加航空座椅零組件的生產，未來航太板材及球拍等產
品亦有機會導入越南製造 。 公司也已著手規劃越南二期廠房 ，
強化海外生產彈性 。 此外 ， 中科后里新廠除作為營運總部及研
發據點外 ， 也將支援新業務開發 。 初期鎖定人形機器人關鍵的
腰部與膝蓋複材結構件與客戶進行共同開發，同時也積極切
入農業與商用無人機 、 低軌衛星等先端應用 。 子公司新鴻洲方

面 ， 則運用精密射出及模具技術 ， 計畫開拓半導體與人形機器

發與客戶認證推進，長期有助於拓展複合材料及精密零組件
的應用領域。

評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上

本次報告：法說會

系統電 (5309 TT) 
2027 年重回成長軌道

永豐觀點 
系統電連三個月營收創下新高，預期在 BBU 及無人載具開始出貨下，營收逐月成
長。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持中立投資建議：系統電在經歷營運轉型整合期後，2027 年營收及獲利將重返
成長 ， 然研究員仍維持中立的投資建議 ， 主係考量(1) 營運槓桿效應須待 2H27 時，
BBU 放量出貨較明顯。此外，系統電為因應未來產能持續擴充 BBU 及無人機產線
侵蝕獲利；(2) 評價上，以 2027 年稀釋後稅後 EPS 2.17 元計算，目前本益比約
30 倍，評價尚屬合理，故維持中立的投資建議，持續關注 BBU 出貨及新訂單狀
況。

系統電營運主力將轉移至 BBU 相關應用： 系統電子工業股份有限公司成立於1977
年，原專注在車用電子、能源、通訊、電玩遊戲。近年公司陸續進行業務調整及轉
型 ， 將業務分為汽車電子 、 工業物聯網 、AI 資料中心能源系統管理三大事業 ，2015
年推出藍牙胎壓偵測器(BLE TPMS)， 目前主要經營業務內容為車用胎壓偵測系統 、
車用電子產品、綠色能源及儲能設備、電源供應器，並獲有多項專利，並持續擴大
BLE TPMS 應用在歐美 OE 前裝市場，如新能源訂動車、大客車車隊、皮卡等。近
期受到資料中心用電需求增加，系統電 BBU 相關產品將開始出貨。1H26 產品比
重：TPMS 46%、工業電腦 38%、AI 資料中心能源系統及其他 16%。 
3Q26 強固型工業電腦出貨旺，：系統電 9 月營收 3.67 億元(+12.04%MoM，
+17.87%YoY)，連續三個月創下歷史新高，主係因強固型電腦產品出貨。雖 BBU
及無人機相關產品尚未大量出貨，然在工業電腦領域，受惠產業趨勢帶動，如農耕
車及無人載具相關應用成長快速，使系統電 3Q26 營收 10.14 億元(+13%QoQ，
+24%YoY)，研究處預估毛利率 23.2%(+1ppt QoQ，+1.9ppt YoY)，因積極投入
研發及新產品設計使營業費用較高，稅後淨利 0.04 億元，稅後 EPS 為 0.02 元。 
4Q26 兩大專案 BBU 及無人機小量出貨 ：先前耕於已久的 BBU 產品將於十月開始
出貨 ， 初期以3kW 產品為主 ， 系統電已完成建置 2 條全自動 與 1 條半自動 BBU
產線。無人機方面，包含軍用及微型軍警偵察需求強勁，產線亦完成建置。研究處
預期 4Q26 營收 11.17 億元(+10.2%QoQ，+28%YoY)，毛利率 24%(+0.8ppt 
QoQ，-0.6ppt YoY)，稅後淨利 0.07 億元，稅後 EPS 為 0.03 元。預估 2026 年營
收 38.9 億元(+14.7%YoY)，毛利率 23.6%(+0.1pts YoY) ，稅後淨利 0.24 億元(-
54.3%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 0.11 元。 
2027 年迎來營運收成期：展望 2027 年，以三大事業體區分，工業電腦方面將聚
焦於邊緣算力模組、GCS 地面控制站，部門產品毛利率最佳，有利於獲利表現；AI
資料中心能源系統方面，BBU 產品將開始大量出貨，並持續擴充產能，預估部門
營收將年增 328%，並將增加 3-4 條 BBU 產線，因應未來新產品的加入；TPMS
業務收到車市影響，營運相對平淡。綜上所述，系統電2027 年營運將開始收成過
去在無人載具及 BBU 電源產品的投入，研究處預期 2027 年營收 61.68 億元
(+58.5%YoY)，毛利率 24.9%(+1.3pts YoY) ，稅後淨利 5.41 億元，稅後 EPS 為
2.36 元。

營運現況與分析

## Document 209: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성전자 (005930)

Company Brief 
반도체/디스플레이 
Analyst 김선우 
sunwoo.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 우서현 
seohyun.woo@meritz.co.kr 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정  
3Q26E 영업이익 106.1조원, 원화강세 불구 견조한 수준 기록 전망 
삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익은 106.1조원으로 낮아진 시장 기대치 (104조원)를 소폭 
능가할 전망이다. 해당 분기동안 급격한 원화강세로 인해 실적 측면의 비우호적 환경
이 조성되었지만, 동사는 강건한 판매 정책에 기반해 메모리 판가가 산업 평균을 재
차 능가하며 견조한 실적 개선이 가능했다. 
동사 메모리 사업부의 3Q26 영업이익은 109조원으로 예상되며, 이중 DRAM과 
NAND는 각각 80조원 (OPM 78.5%) 과 29조원 (OPM 68.9%) 을 차지할 전망이다. 
DRAM과 NAND 출하량은 기존 예상치를 소폭 하회하는 +3.4%/+4.2% QoQ을 기
록하겠지만, 판가가 무려 +20.1%/19.0% QoQ 상승하며 실적 개선을 이끌겠다. 전
분기에 이어 ‘DS 특별경영성과급’ 충당금이 반영되며 손익에 일부 영향을 미치겠지
만, 동사 특유의 ‘수익성 극대화’ 판가 정책이 산업 우위 구도를 조성한다. 한편 LSI/
파운드리는 영업손실이 1.5조원을 기록하며 적자가 지속될 전망이다. 가동률 개선에 
기반한 사업 회복이 진행중이지만, 테일러팹 가동과 함께 비용 요인이 지속 출회할 
전망이다. 한편 반도체 외 기타 사업부의 험난한 실적 추이는 지속될 전망이다. SDC 
1.1조원 영업이익을 제외하고, DX (세트) 부문의 경우 MX -1.9조원, DA/VD -7,400
억원으로 영업적자 확대되리라 예상된다. 당사가 지난해 말부터 주장했듯, 세트 사업
은 메모리 등 부품원가 비중이 급격히 상승하는 가운데 향후 ‘판가 인상 → 판매량 
축소’의 ‘내구재화’ 변모 속 적자 최소화 노력만이 불가피할 전망이다.

1Q27 일부 모바일 DRAM 판가 +50% 폭등 예상. 적극적 매수 권고 
AGI를 향한 경쟁적이며 구조적 서버 수요 증가 불구, 스마트폰 중심의 B2C 응용처
는 유일한 판가저항처로 남아있다. 하지만 당사의 채널체크에 따르면, 메모리 업체들
의 지속적인 생산 전환 (LPDDR → 서버 DRAM 및 HBM)에 기반해 주요 고객 향 
모바일 메모리 판가는 무려 1Q27부터 50% 이상 QoQ 상향될 전망이다. 주요 고객
은 내년 20주년 플래그십 스마트폰을 제작하는 상황에서 판가 폭등을 감내해야하는 
상황이다. 최근 메모리 사이클 개선 속도에 대한 막연하며 진부한 우려가 존재하나, 
향후 1~2개월간 메모리 업체들에 대한 2027년 실적 추정치 상향과 함께 ‘성장·저평
가·주주환원 매력’이 동시에 발생할 전망이다. 동사의 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 기
존의 562조원에서 632조원으로 13% 상향조정하며, 시장 눈높이 역시 지속 상향되
리라 예상한다. 한편 배당 중심으로 발표됐던 동사의 주주환원의 경우, 모든 주주에게 
더 실효적인 ‘자사주 매입·소각’ 방안이 병행되는 방향으로 변모하리라 예상된다. 투
자의견 Buy와 적정주가 50만원을 유지한다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 7

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 500,000원 
현재주가 (10.6) 272,000원 
상승여력 83.8%

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다 
기다렸던 4 행정 중속 엔진 캐파 증설 단행 
HD현대중공업의 4행정 엔진 캐파는 기존 3.0GW에서 증설이 완료되는 '28년부터 
램프업을 거쳐 '30년 7.2GW까지 확대될 계획이다. ①[육상 발전용]은 신공장(울산 
온산) 3GW 와 HD 현대엔진(전남 영암) 1GW 를 합쳐 4.0GW, ②[선박용]은 
2.3GW에서 3.2GW로 확대된다. HD현대엔진 캐파는 기존 0.7GW에서 1GW까
지 확대되며, 해당 라인은 신공장이 가동되기 전 ‘27~’28 년 구간에서의 선박&육상 엔
진 모두 대응 가능하다. 신공장 캐파는 총 3.0GW로, ‘28년 약 30%의 가동률로 시
작해 램프업을 거쳐 ‘30 년부터 풀캐파로 20MW급 모델을 생산할 예정이다. 4 행정 
중속엔진에 대한 수요 가시성은 높다. 동사가 공유했던 육상 발전 엔진 인콰이어리 규
모는 기존 약 3~9GW였으나, 현재는 이를 크게 상회하는 수준으로 소통하고 있다.

최근 부유식 데이터센터(FDC)에 대한 하이퍼스케일러들의 인콰이어리 또한 확대되고 
있다. HD 한국조선해양은 최근 미국 선급(ABS)로부터 100MW급 규모의 부유식 발
전 설비(BMPP)에 대한 기본 인증(AiP)를 획득했으며, 동사는 FDC 신조 사업과 더불
어 발전원 4 행정 엔진을 파워십 및 부유식 발전설비(BMPP)에 납품하며 그룹사들과 
패키지 사업으로 진행할 예정이다. 가시화된 수요를 기반으로 엔진기계사업부의 구조적 
성장 보여줄 것으로 전망하며, 이에 따른 리레이팅을 예상한다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 및 목표주가 기존 97 만원 유지 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’ 및 목표주가 기존 97 만원을 유지한다. 동사는 
올해 상선수주 목표 이미 초과달성하며 안정적인 펀더멘털 유지 중이다. 하반기 남은 
기간은 LNGC, VLGC/VLAC 중심의 선별 수주 전략을 통해 ‘28 년 이후의 상선 부
문 수익성 유지 충분히 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 글로벌 지정학적 리스크 확대에 따른 
해군 현대화 사이클 기조에 맞추어 미 해군은 동맹국의 호위함 구매를 고려하고 있으
며, MASGA를 기반으로 중장기적으로 미국 전투함을 건조하기 위해 HD현대의 미국 
진출 가능성 높다는 판단이다. 또한 미국을 제외한 글로벌 함정(페루, 필리핀, 말레이시
아, 칠레, 사우디, 이집트 등) 수주 파이프라인도 다수 보유하고 있어 함정 모멘텀에 따
른 리레이팅 충분히 가능할 전망이다. 특수선+4 행정 엔진+FDC+SMR 등 조선소에 
붙일 수 있는 모든 추가 성장동력을 보유한 유일한 종목이기에 조선업 최선호주(Top-
Pick)로 제시한다. 최근 불거지고 있는 노조 파업 문제 해소될 시 저점 매수 기회라는 
판단이다.

Editorial 
A Fed credibility-induced bounce  
Strategy summary:  The Fed’s unanimous decision to hike 
rates this week for the first time since 2023 , and pencil in 
another hike by December, confirms our expectations – flagged 
in the last two weeklies - of a central bank willing to establish its 
independence against political pressure. The Fed’s moderately 
hawkish pivot is helping restore its inflation-fighting credentials. 
The BoJ also hiked rates this week. It is the first time on record 
all three major central banks – Fed, ECB and BoJ – have hiked 
in the same month , as they respond primarily to a resurgence 
in energy-driven inflation amid tight job markets.  
We expect the Fed to hike once more by year-end to 4.0-4.25% 
and follow up with another hike in H1 2027 to 4.25 -4.5%. With 
the revised estimates, we revise up our US government bond 
yield forecasts. Nevertheless, with Fed policy uncertainty 
easing, we see an opportunity to use the equity market dip to 
add exposure amid solid corporate earnings.  
Fed moves to restore credibility:  The Fed’s policy pivot this 
week followed Chair Warsh’s hawkish Jackson Hole speech 
and recent economic data prints, namely: a pick -up in job 
creation and inflation over the summer and a sharp rebound in 
oil prices due to the re -escalation of the Middle E ast conflict. 
Given the unanimous Fed decision to hike rates this week, we 
expect two more hikes by  June 2027 on the back of  robust 
growth amid strong AI investments and a tight labour market, 
keeping inflation elevated. We expect this pivot to help the Fed 
rebuild credibility as an inflation fighter.  
Policy outlook less aggressive than market pricing : The 
updated Fed policy rate estimates are lower compared with 
money market pricing of three more rate hikes by June 2027 
since we expect disinflation to take hold by Q2 2027 as the 
impact of oil prices and tariffs fade. We believe policy rates can 
return to the 4.0–4.25% range by end-2027, with the possibility 
of cuts in H2 2027 as inflation pressures ease.  
Upgrading near-term US rates and USD estimates : Based 
on these revised estimates, we also upgrade our 3-month target 
for the US 10 -year government bond yield to 5.0 -5.25%. We 
also revise up our 12 -month target for the US 10 -year bond 
yield to 4.75-5.0% (see page 4).  
Buying the equity dip as focus turns to fundamentals: Fed 
hikes typically do not hurt equities over the medium-term when 
accompanied by strong earnings growth. We estimate a 25bps 
rise in Fed rates (the discount rate) would impact the S&P500 
index and global equities by 3 -4%. However, we expect the 
headwind from higher rates to be offset by strong earnings 
growth, driving positive performance for global equities.  
Furthermore, a hawkish Fed is largely priced in and we expect 
the Fed to deliver fewer rate hikes than currently priced by 
markets. Meanwhile, we believe concerns over AI industry 
leaders slowing the pace of development are overdone. Hence, 
we maintain our preference for a diversified group of large cap 
technology and semiconductor industry leaders (see page 5-6). 
BoJ hikes; modestly bullish JPY : The BoJ hiked rates by 
25bps to a three-decade high of 1.25%, as expected, but the 7-
2 split vote led to renewed JPY weakness on concerns it may 
slow the pace of further hikes. We remain constructive on JPY 
amid narrowing rate differential vs peers as the BoJ potentially 
hikes rates by 25bps once per quarter until Q2 2027 to curb 
rising domestic wage-driven inflation pressure . Any sign of BoJ 
slowing the pace of hikes could lead to renewed JPY weakness, 
fuelling inflationary pressures (see page 7).  
BoE holds; rangebound GBP: The BoE held rates this week, 
bucking the hiking trend among peers, as weakening UK job 
and housing markets offset rebound in energy-driven inflation. 
We expect BoE to deliver fewer rate hikes than the 100bps of 
increase currently priced by markets  as the job market slows 
further. This is likely to keep GBP rangebound (see page 7).  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions 
  Where do you see US government bond  yields following 
the Fed’s 16 September decision to hike interest rates? 
Our view: The US 10-year government bond yield is likely to 
retest the 5.00-5.25% range in the near term. We favour 3-7-year 
maturity bond s in t he US, preferring credit over government 
bonds. We are looking for an opportunity to extend duration 
approaching Q2 2027. 
Rationale: Over the next few months, we expect 2- and 30-year US 
government bond yields to remain rangebound, but the 10-year yield 
is expected to rebound up to the 5.00-5.25% range. The reasons for 
this include: 1) oil prices staying elevated and potentially rise further 
due to the fluid geopolitical situation in the Middle East, with Saudi 
Arabia’s East-West oil pipeline shut, the Houthis now firmly in control 
of the Strait of Bab-al-Mandab, the Strait of Hormuz still closed and 
China returning as a buyer of crude oil in the market ; 2) despite 
notable AI leaders expressing the need to slow AI development, AI 
capex plans are showing no signs of slowing down, which continues 
to drive above-trend growth in the economy. This, in turn, also means 
AI-related heavy bond issuance  is expected to continue. These 
issuers typically issue long-dated tenors, and this competition for 
capital will also exert upward pressure on the US 10-year yields. 
We note that the US 10-year bond yield has room to move lower, 
driven by short-covering activities in the very near term as net-short 
positions had moved to extreme levels before th is week’s  Fed 
meeting. However, we think such downshifts would be short-lived 
and the central tendency for the 10-year yield would be to move back 
up to the 5.00-5.25% range. 
Meanwhile, we see US 30-year yields continuing to reflect the dire 
fiscal outlook for the US government. We expect US 2-year yields 
to continue trading in a range that reflects expectations of further 
federal funds rate hikes. For the record, we only expect two more 
rate hikes from the Fed into the middle of 2027 , while money 
markets are pricing in three more for the same period. 
Going into H2 2027, we expect oil prices to moderate (either 
through a resolution of the M iddle East conflict or a re-routing of oil 
supply chains)  and the inflationary effects from tariffs to fade. 
Inflation by then is likely be close to – if not at – the Fed’s target of 
2%. At this point, we see room for the Fed to contemplate rate 
cuts. US 2-year yields (the tenor that is most sensitive to Fed policy 
changes) will likely start to price th is in. With oil prices and inflation 
cooling, 10-year yields would also have room to come down. 
US 30-year yields, on the other hand, are expected to  remain 
elevated as the US fiscal deficit remains substantial next year and 
could become much worse than its already dire state today. As a 
result, the ‘term premium ’ may keep the ultra -long end elevated. 
Short of some unorthodox policy to artificially cap them lower, 30 -
year yields could continue to drift higher into H2 2027. 
We continue to favour the 3-7-year segment of the yield curve and 
prefer credit over government bond s. We see an opportunity for 
us to extend duration closer to Q2 2027. 
—  Jonathan Liang, CFA, CIO for Fixed Income and FX

for US government bond yields

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Will the step -up in the  Fed’s risk-free rate trigger a re -
pricing of equities? 
Our view: We expect strong corporate earnings growth to offset 
the headwind of higher rates , driving positive performance for 
global equities. We remain Overweight global equities. 
Rationale: The Fed’s latest 25bps rate hike had been anticipated 
by the bond market, partly reflected in the bond yield  rise we have 
seen so far in 2026. Using the US 10-year government bond yield as 
a proxy for the risk -free rate, the yield has risen by 80-85bps this 
year. We estimate that each 25bps rise in the discount rate presents 
a valuation drag on global equities. On this basis, the rise in yields 
represents a potential 10-14% headwind for equities this year. 
However, the Fed’s hike reflects a solid expansion of US economic 
activity, resilient domestic spending and robust capital investment. 
This is consistent with the positive earnings revisions we have seen 
this year, with  2026 earnings growth projections now at 34% (and 
16% for 2027). Global equities are up 14% year-to-date (YTD), but 
even with a 14% valuation headwind from higher yields, we believe 
they can rise further, given strong earnings growth in 2026 and 2027. 
—  Fook Hien Yap, Senior Investment Strategist 
  What is the outlook for the US 10-year government bond 
yield? What is your tactical view on the DXY? 
Our view: We raise our three-month US 10 -year government 
bond yield forecast to the 5.00-5.25% range. Meanwhile, we see 
a near-term upward bias in the DXY after its recent downtrend, 
supported by recent Fed tightening and higher  short-term US 
bond yields.  
Rationale: The Fed 's decision  to raise the target range for the 
federal funds rate by 25bps to 3.75-4.00% validates the upside policy 
risks we flagged recently, following Fed Chair Warsh's Jackson Hole 
speech and a run of firmer US economic data. We expect growth 
to remain robust, the labour market  to remain tight and inflation to 
stay elevated, supporting one more hike before year-end 2026 and 
potentially another in H1 2027  – fewer than the three additional 
hikes currently priced in by money markets . For the US 10-year 
government bond yield, further policy tightening and lingering US 
fiscal concerns should keep yields elevated, but the Fed’s visible 
response to inflation should limit additional upward pressure, leaving 
the 10-year yield holding around the 5.00-5.25% range. 
Crucially, Warsh provided no forward guidance on further rate 
hikes. Markets had already priced in substantial additional rate hikes 
ahead of the Fed meeting, reducing the scope for further USD upside 
purely from rate repricing. Meanwhile, we expect further policy 
tightening from the ECB and the BoJ, while the RBA also remains 
biased towards further tightening. This should gradually narrow the 
US rate advantage and limit the extent of a DXY rebound. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Anthony Naab, CFA, Investment Strategist

Global equities’ earnings growth estimates have 
been revised higher this year, offsetting the 
headwind from higher rates

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  How should investors position in technology as AI lab 
CEOs call for a slowdown in frontier AI model development? 
Our view: We believe concerns over “pacing the frontier” are 
overdone. We maintain our preference to remain invested in 
and diversified across Big Tech and semiconductor leaders. 
Rationale: We believe it is prudent for investors to avoid 
overreacting to the recent calls for a slowdown in frontier AI model 
development, as they focus on responsible AI development, rather 
than halting AI model development or reducing spending. The global 
AI leadership  race, reinforced by the US and China’s  strategic 
ambitions, also makes a coordinated slowdown unlikely. 
While the near-term narrative may shift from AI training beneficiaries 
towards inference plays, hyperscalers continue to signal strong AI 
infrastructure commitments and improving monetisation, supporting 
our AI capex outlook. Rising AI adoption and agentic use cases 
should sustain inference demand, even if training activity moderates, 
benefiting custom AI chips, NAND flash memory and hyperscalers.  
We believe the decline in valuations  this year more than 
compensates for any downside risk to AI capex. Our Opportunistic 
idea on Global Semiconductors further reflects our conviction, with 
attractive valuations supporting selective additions during volatility. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
  With ASEAN and UK equities outperforming AI-focused 
markets in H2 2026, is it worth adding to these markets? 
Our view: ASEAN and UK equities offer portfolio diversification, 
but we are Underweight both markets in favour of US and Asia 
ex-Japan (AxJ) equities, given their stronger earnings potential. 
Rationale: The ASEAN and UK markets have outperformed the 
MSCI ACWI over the past three months, but both have 
underperformed on a YTD basis. Financials is the largest sector in 
the ASEAN (53%) and the UK (28%) and provides steady earnings. 
However, both markets have minimal exposure to the technology 
sector (just 3% and 1% , respectively), ultimately dragging down 
growth. Hence, while defensive markets offer value by increasing 
diversification and reducing volatility in a portfolio, we maintain our 
Underweight allocation for ASEAN and the UK, as we see much 
stronger earnings potential elsewhere in the US and AxJ markets.  
We remain Overweight US and AxJ, supported by robust earnings 
growth, as  AI still has the potential to become a major economic 
growth driver . Our 12-month S&P500 Index forecast of 8,400  
implies low-teens upside, supported by a strong earnings backdrop 
of 25-30% earnings-per-share growth for 2026 and 15 -20% for 
2027. AxJ also boasts the strongest earnings growth among major 
markets, fuelled by the AI semiconductor super-cycle. 
—  Cindy Lam, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist

Our AI capex estimates are 15-20% below 
consensus expectations to account for execution 
risks

### Page 7

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Why is it still appropriate to have a positive stance on US 
high-yield (HY) bonds? 
Our view: We remain opportunistically bullish on US HY bonds. 
Lower sensitivity to interest rate volatility, a below-trend default 
rate and a maturity wall pushed out to 2028-29 drive this view. 
Rationale: Recent interest rate volatility has highlighted a key 
structural advantage of US HY bonds: their higher coupon rates  
and shorter tenors relative to Developed Market (DM) government 
bonds and US investment-grade (IG) corporate bonds translate into 
a much lower sensitivity to interest rates, cushioning bond prices 
during yield backup episodes, where bond yields rise sharply. 
YTD, US HY has delivered 1.8% in total returns, outperforming DM 
government bonds and US IG corporate bonds by over 3% each on 
a relative basis. This outperformance was particularly evident during 
the recent back-up in bond yields, as inflation concerns re-emerged 
after renewed Middle East tensions – an environment where HY's 
lower rate sensitivity provided a relative cushion  even as broader 
fixed income markets came under pressure in absolute terms. On 
the fundamentals side, US HY corporate credit quality remains 
resilient. Default rates continue to track below long-run trend levels, 
and the maturity wall has been pushed out to 2028 -29, reducing 
near-term refinancing risk. Against a non -recessionary macro 
backdrop, we expect these credit fundamentals to hold, supporting 
the case for continued opportunistic exposure to the asset class.  
—  Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Do the latest BoJ and BoE meetings alter your view on the 
central banks’ rate paths and their corresponding currencies? 
Our view: We remain constructive on the JPY and expect two 
BoJ rate hikes this year and potentially one hike per quarter in 
H1 2027 as policy normalisation continues. Meanwhile, the BoE 
held rates at 3.75%. We expect GBP/USD to stay rangebound 
within 1.32-1.36 over the next 1-3 months, with downside risk 
towards the lower end of our medium-term 1.30-1.36 range. 
Rationale: Persistent inflation, higher energy prices and earlier JPY 
weakness support further BoJ policy normalisation . Continued 
hiking should progressively narrow the US -Japan rate gap, reduce 
the attractiveness of JPY -funded carry trades and support  the JPY 
over the medium term towards the 153-155 range. However, with a 
September hike largely priced  in, future tightening ’s pace  will be 
more important for the JPY. Meanwhile, the BoE was split 6-3 in its 
decision to hold rates at 3.75% , with three members preferring a 
25bps hike, signalling elevated inflation risks. However, a weaker 
labour market is likely to limit the pace of BoE tightening. The US-
UK rate divergence is expected to favour the USD over the next 1-3 
months. Further BoE tightening (if inflation remains elevated ) could 
narrow the rate gap and provide support to the GBP later in the cycle. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist

We expect US HY bonds to continue delivering 
strong performance in the near term

## Document 244: ASM大涨！2027年营收目标上调24%，宣布最高3亿欧元股票回购计划｜财报见闻

Source PDF: all report pdf/ASM大涨！2027年营收目标上调24%，宣布最高3亿欧元股票回购计划｜财报见闻.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ASM 第三季度营收 11.6 亿欧元，同⽐增⻓ 44% ，净利润为 3.1 亿欧元，两项指标均超出市场
预期。公司上调 2027 年营收⽬标约 24% ⾄逾 57 亿欧元，此前 7 ⽉份预测为 46 亿欧元以上。
公司还宣布了最⾼ 3 亿欧元的股票回购计划。 CEO 表⽰市场需求⾮常强劲，客⼾正在加速扩
产能。 ASM ADR ⼤涨 7% 。
摘要：
ASM International ⼤幅上调明年营收预期， AI 基础设施建设带动的旺盛需求正推动这家荷兰芯
⽚设备制造商或将提前三年完成 2030 年增⻓⽬标。
周⼆，ASM International 将 2027 年营收⽬标上调约 24% ⾄逾 57 亿欧元（约 63 亿美元，按固定
汇率计算），⾼于 7 ⽉份预测的 46 亿欧元以上。
从盈利表现来看， ASM 第三季度营收按固定汇率计算同⽐增⻓ 44% ⾄ 11.6 亿欧元，净利润为
3.081 亿欧元，两项指标均超出分析师平均预期。
公司同步公布了最⾼ 3 亿欧元的股票回购计划。消息公布后， ASM 的美国存托凭证⼤涨 7% ，报

838.9 美元。
⾸席执⾏官 Hichem M'Saad 表⽰， " 需求环境依然⾮常强劲，客⼾产能持续紧张，并正加速提升
产出。 "
AI 数据中⼼投资潮，推动上下游同步扩张
ASM 明年增⻓的核⼼动⼒来⾃存储芯⽚及先进逻辑芯⽚制造商的双重需求拉动，两类芯⽚均是
数据中⼼运营商建设 AI 基础设施的关键原材料。
ASM 的核⼼产品为薄膜沉积设备，⽤于在晶圆表⾯沉积极薄的功能膜层，是芯⽚制造流程中的
关键⼯序。随着客⼾加速扩产并寻求提升芯⽚效率与性能， ASM 正持续赢得更多订单。
ASM 的客⼾群涵盖台积电与英特尔等⾏业巨头。台积电负责为英伟达和苹果⽣产芯⽚，其资本
⽀出计划⾼达 640 亿美元；英特尔则预计今年在新⼚房及设备上投⼊约 200 亿美元。

and the Euro crisis. But in an historical perspective, today’s spreads are only high by the

France has also seen its debt rise in relative terms. As recently as the GFC in 2008, France’s
debt-to-GDP was basically in line with Germany’s and well below Italy’s, but today it’s far
above Germany’s and closing in on Italy’s. Outside of the temporary Covid spike, Italian
debt/GDP has been stable for over a decade.

And this isn’t just about France, as Italy’s spread has also widened in recent sessions. However,
unlike France, the Italian spread isn’t at its highest since 2012.

► 隨人工智慧運算需求持續攀升 ，谷歌、亞馬

優化成 本 。根據 凱 基投顧 引述產 業數 據預
估，2027年客製化晶片出貨量將年增 92%至
1,446萬顆 ，首度超 越繪圖處理器 (GPU)的
1,129萬顆 ，顯示AI晶片市場正由 GPU主導
逐步轉向多元化發展。
► 在客製化晶片方面 ，谷歌持續擴大自研張量
處理器 (TPU)的應用與對外供應 ，亞馬遜亦
積極拓展自研AI晶片(Trainium)的雲端運算服
務，預估兩者合計將佔 2027年客製化晶片出
貨量約80%。
► 隨客製 化晶 片需 求 快速增 加 ，相 關晶 片設
計、先 進製 程及 先 進封裝 需求可 望同 步提
升，涉及上述業務發展的台股供應鏈 (註)有機
會受到市場關注。

### Page 6

6
投資焦點
► 2024年全球工廠運作的工業機器人約 470萬
台，為10年前的 3倍，主要為韓國 、中國和
日本等製造業大國將其運用於汽車 、電子和
金屬加工等產業 ，並因而維持成本和品質優
勢，為競爭力重要來源之一 。雖然美國工業
機器人的普及程度仍低於亞洲主要製造業國
家，但近年積極尋求重建工業產能 、將關鍵
供應鏈遷回美國國內以及擴大半導體和製藥
生產，有可能讓美國逐步調整工業和製造層
面的策略方向。
► 傳統工業機器人與人形機器人在外觀設計與
應用場景上有明顯差異 。工業機器人通常部
署於高度標準化的環境 ，以執行範圍相對有
限且重複性高的特定作業為主 ，而人形機器
人則被設計用於多變的環境 ，並經常需要與
人類協同工作 ，因此需結合移動能力 、感知
能力與靈巧操作能力 ，才能夠執行多樣化任
務，而非受限單一功能。
工業機器人技術相對成熟且部署速度較快全球運作中的工業機器人數量穩定成長
工業機器人技術成熟且穩定成長，為製造業強國的重要競爭力來源

### Page 7

7
投資焦點人形機器人
► 人 形機 器 人 主 要由 結 合半 導 體 與AI 模 型的
「大腦」，並由感測器 、執行器 、線材、連
接器及輕量化電池驅動的機身所組成 ，可透
過感知環境資訊 、思考並進行協調的實體動
作。投行預估至2030年，執行器將佔整體人
形機器人成本超過 50%，為價值含量最高的
核心零組件 ，麥肯錫公司甚至認為執行器與
感測器可能成為未來供應鏈中的關鍵瓶頸。
► 機器人在實體世界中執行任務時 ，任何延遲
或連線中斷都可能帶來重大風險 ，因此相較
純數位的 AI應用 ，對即時運算 (邊緣運算 )能
力的要求更高 。而近幾年邊緣運算軟硬體能
力大幅提升 ，使本地端設備得以部署數十億
參數的 AI模型 ，並將反應延遲縮短至約 0.3
秒，使機器人能夠即時感知 、決策與行動 。
此外，過去6年風險資本對機器人、感測器及
IT 硬體的每年平均投資約 100 億美元 ， 但
2026年僅上半年即已達 280億美元 ，暗示市
場對機器人產業潛力的期待有別於過往。
機器人技術進展快速並開始吸引風險資本投資人形機器人運作有賴AI模型與大量機身零組件整合

人形機器人產業結合AI與硬體，含金量高吸引風險資本進駐

### Page 8

8
股市
◆ 美、歐、日央行升息抑通膨 ，公債殖利率走高壓抑股債市場近期動能 。然而包括油價自高檔回落使
通膨壓力稍緩 ，以及美股第三季獲利年增率仍為雙位數成長 ，股市表現有支撐 。市場持續觀察AI資
本支出是否帶來獲利成長 ，短期內評價面壓力將增添市場動盪 ，可分散配置於不同國家與產業 ，降
低市場波動影響 。總結上述，地緣衝突、殖利率偏高及評價面壓力等紛擾帶來負面影響 ，可布局防
禦股抗震，長期而言，科技及AI半導體發展趨勢不變且基本面佳，可逢拉回時分批布局。
◆ 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，加上全球利率走揚有利銀行股 。此外，地緣衝突頻傳 ，歐洲國防軍
工仍有投資題材。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，中長
期看好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分
可關注金融產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、歐洲國防
債市
◆ 全球公債殖利率攀升 ，且最新聯準會利率點陣圖顯示 ，官員預估今年底前仍有一碼升息空間 ，立場
偏向維持較高利率水平抑制通膨發展 ，加上9月製造業及服務業PMI優於預期，殖利率偏高環境建議

以中短天期債券投資為主 ，可逢利率走升時布局進行鎖利領息 。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流
與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
◆ 可以非美貨幣債券如歐元或澳幣計價投資級債券，分散單一貨幣風險。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、科技、通訊及公用事業等
衛星配置：非美貨幣債券分散風險
匯市
◆ 聯準會升息且態度偏鷹，美元高檔盤整。
◆ 日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，未如市場預期的鷹派，日圓震盪。
美元高檔整理，日圓震盪
商品
◆ 中東地緣衝突加劇推升油價，短期紛擾難解，偏高的油價可能對通膨及實體經濟帶來影響。
◆ 短期受美國雙率走高有壓 ，惟美日財政赤字壓力與去美元化使全球央行買盤依舊 ，提供黃金中長期
穩定支撐。
短期黃金有壓，中長期維持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產
策略思維
策略思維 短期添防禦，AI與科技逢拉回布局，透過多元配置降低短期影響

### Page 15

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

### Page 2

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The six-month annualised growth rate in the Westpac–
Melbourne Institute Leading Index, which indicates the
likely pace of economic activity relative to trend three to
nine months into the future, held at –0.03% in September,
unchanged from August.

The Leading Index continues to show moderately improved
momentum compared to the middle of the year, with the
latest reads pointing to growth holding at or slightly below
trend heading into late 2026/early 2027. Growth appears
to be stabilising at a slow pace rather than stalling. That
said, both the latest fuel prices and the RBA’s September
interest rate increase are still working their way through to
the economy with impacts likely to take some of the gloss off

growth near term. Overall, Westpac expects annual growth
to come in at 1.5% for calendar 2026, a below-trend result.
The Leading Index growth rate has firmed slightly over the
last six months, rising from –0.16% back in March to –0.03%
in September. The component detail shows mixed moves over
the period. Four components have contributed more positively
with reduced drags from the Westpac-Melbourne Institute
Unemployment Expectations Index (adding +0.23ppts to

the Index growth rate); the Westpac-Melbourne Institute
Consumer Expectations Index (adding another +0.15ppts);
and the S&P/ASX200 (+0.15ppts) combining with a modest
lift in growth in US industrial production (adding +0.12ppts).
These positives have been partially offset by modest drags
from four previously stable components: dwelling approvals
(–0.34ppts); monthly hours worked (–0.08ppts); commodity
prices, measured in AUD terms (–0.07ppts) and the yield

spread (–0.05ppts).
Overall, the component mix speaks to a lack of direction.
Indeed, despite some large shocks over the last year, the
Leading Index growth rate has been relatively contained since
early 2025, holding in a narrow –0.3% to +0.5% range. This
compares to history which, outside of the COVID and GFC
shocks, has seen growth rates move between a much wider
–1.9% to +1.6% range. Of course, the apparent stability,
which is evident across all components of the index, may not

last but for now it suggests the economy has been stuck in a
bit of a holding pattern over the last two years.
Leading Index still tracking a touch below trend
The Westpac-Melbourne Institute Leading Index of
Economic Activity is designed to assist in identifying
turning points in the economy. The index combines
variables that reflect different aspects of the economy
into a single index that generally produces a more reliable
cyclical indicator than any single component taken

individually. The Leading Index of Economic Activity
provides advance information on the state of the economy
and gives early warnings of cyclical turning points.
Matthew Hassan
Head of Australian Macro-Forecasting
**Please note that this will be the last Westpac press release for
the monthly Westpac-Melbourne Institute Leading Index. We will
continue to publish regular bulletin updates through our westpaciq.
com.au website.

議，將⾃  2027 年 1 ⽉ 1 ⽇起正式向⼤客戶供應⾼階半導體封裝載板（三星电机
与全球⼤型企业签订 “ 存储器半导体⽤封装基板 ” ⻓期供应合同（ LTA ））。報導

指出，在  28 億美元注資總承諾中，輝達預計出資約  2.6 兆韓元，特斯拉出資
約  1.1 兆韓元。這筆巨額資⾦旨在為輝達下⼀代  Blackwell Ultra GB300
NVL72 超級計算叢集、 Rubin 架構  GPU ，以及特斯拉即將量產的  HW5 ⾃動
駕駛⾞載晶⽚與  Optimus 機器⼈神經網絡晶⽚，鎖定最為緊缺的⾼層數、⼤
⾯積先進封裝載板產能。

DRAM ， NAND 與三星電⼦的採購關係，擴⼤到三星電機  ABF 載板產能擴充

製造整合服務來搶台廠份額。
未獲得載板保證的⼆線  ASIC 晶⽚設計公司：頂級載板產能被巨頭包攬，使資
⾦鏈較弱的中⼩  AI 晶⽚公司⾯臨載板交期延⻑⾄  50 週以上的斷鏈危機。
Our outside the box non-consensus thinking
特斯拉參投載板是「實體  AI 製造去台化 / 去⽇化」的戰略預演：市場多將注意

⼒集中於輝達，但特斯拉⾸次拿出  1.1 兆韓元直投載板廠更具震撼性。這證明
⾺斯克已深刻意識到 ——HW5 晶⽚與百萬台  Optimus 的真正死⽳不僅僅是
晶圓代⼯產能短缺，⽽也包括 ABF 封裝載板⾯積及厚度激增引發的良率瓶頸。
特斯拉直接以⾞企⾝分下場穿透供應鏈投資底層零組件，正在重構半導體採購
範式。
「晶⽚⾯積膨脹」倒逼載板成為半導體新護城河：隨著晶⽚尺⼨光罩極限朝
10x 以上突破（ Reticle Limit ），單顆  GB300 或  HW5 載板尺⼨已超過
100mm x 100mm ，相當於⼀塊⼩型主機板。載板製造已從傳統  PCB 加⼯
轉變為「類晶圓級微影⼯程」，載板產能的鎖定甚⾄⽐搶奪台積電  CoWoS 配
額更具決定性。

### Page 3

Our advises to investors
我們建議投資⼈關注三星電機（ Samsung Electro-Mechanics ）及其核⼼材
料設備供應商在產業份額的變化，尤其是在輝達與特斯拉雙重認證下，其⾼階
載板營收佔⽐將在未來兩年由不⾜  15% 是否能飆升⾄  30% 以上，迎來估值
重估。適度獲利了結估值處於歷史⾼點的傳統純載板標的，防範  2027 年三星
電機新產能開出後，⾏業整體⽑利率⾯臨均值回歸的競爭壓⼒。三星電機產線
在⼤⾯積  FC-BGA 的翹曲控制（ Warpage Control ）與熱膨脹良率爬坡仍值
得觀察。

## Document 347: Open mouth operations (2026-10-28)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Open mouth operations (2026-10-28).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-28
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#australia #luciellis #rba
Open mouth operations
By Luci Ellis
Chief Economist Westpac Group, Westpac
13:34 October 23 2026 Share
The RBA does not want to promise what it will do, but it is increasingly trying to tell us what
it thinks.
Central banks’ communication is carefully scrutinised for every nuance. Lately, there seems to have
been more RBA communication to pore over. The method of delivery has also evolved. This has

### Page 2

Market participants pore over every utterance by central banks for clues to future rate moves. They will
have plenty to pore over in the run-up to the November RBA decision. After the flurry of fireside chats
this week, ’60 Minutes’ will run a pre-recorded interview with chief economist Sarah Hunter on Sunday.
Next Wednesday, the whole senior policy team will appear before Senate Estimates, just as the
quarterly CPI is released. (This timing will have been the Senate’s choice, not the RBA’s.) After the

decision will come the Statement on Monetary Policy, a media release and press conference. More
speeches will follow, including one by the Deputy Governor on the Thursday.
RBA communication does seem to have changed, not just following the RBA Review but also more
recently. There may be fewer policy meetings each year, but the communication outside that certainly
feels more frequent than a couple of years ago. Back in 2024, speaking engagements felt a little

sparse outside the post-meeting media conference and parliamentary appearances. We have counted
58 public engagements so far this year on the Speeches page on the RBA’s website. This compares
with 58 for the whole of last year and 52 in 2024. Excluding the ‘compulsory’ press conferences and
parliamentary appearances, fewer than half of the 2024 events were related to economics, markets
and monetary policy.
The ‘how’ has changed
The way the RBA communicates with markets has changed as well. There are fewer set-piece

speeches on policy and a lot more semi-scripted ‘fireside chats’. Compared with a few years ago, there
are more interviews across a wide range of media. This is a big change from the pre-Review world. It
was a huge deal when the previous Governor appeared on the 7:30 Report; it is hard to imagine him
agreeing to an Assistant Governor appearing on 60 Minutes.
Part of the change reflects increased efforts to reach a general audience. Central banks want to explain

why they do what they do. This matters especially when interest rates are rising but the household
sector is not the source of the inflation.
Speaking differently to different audiences can sometimes confuse markets. As the RBA’s Chief
Communication Officer highlighted in a recent panel discussion, the post-meeting media conference is
designed to speak to the public. Market participants and economists certainly tune in, but we are not
sometimes caused confusion when tone seems to vary. Different audiences call for different tone and

varying complexity.
The content has evolved, too. RBA officials are increasingly using speeches directed to professional
audiences to discuss more technical estimates of inferred variables such as neutral rates and
financial conditions. They are also highlighting that monetary policy combats inflation in more ways
than just squeezing mortgage holders.
One thing has not changed: the RBA is not in the business of providing ‘forward guidance’ promises

about the future path of interest rates. It does, however, give more running commentary about how it
is interpreting recent data. These interpretations are themselves useful signals about what the RBA
thinks it might do.

### Page 3

the primary audience. So if you think that what the Governor said in the media conference sounded
more ‘dovish’ than what another official says in a more market-oriented forum, it is partly because she
is trying to convey that the RBA understands that its recent policy actions are making things harder for
many Australians. But this is in much the same way that a doctor might say, “This might hurt a bit.” It
does, but that will not stop the treatment.

A related issue is that it is easier to control tone in pre-drafted material such as the minutes and set-
piece speeches than in unstructured Q&A. Senior central bankers are trained to be careful with their
words in unscripted settings. If the tone differs, the minutes are still more likely to say what the Board
really means than an off-the-cuff answer.
Greater communication with the broader public might have reduced the pressure to make the set-piece

speeches more accessible. Research on central bank communication often uses measures such as
the Flesch–Kincaid Grade Level and Flesch Reading Ease score. A comprehensive analysis was
beyond the scope of this note. However, a spot comparison suggests four speeches from 2026 (1, 2, 3,
4) were harder to read than four from 2021 (1, 2, 3, 4). The 2021 speeches scored in the 11 to 12
range, which is high-school-level complexity. The 2026 speeches scored between 12 and 15, which is

academic or technically dense text requiring university-level education. (Including two of my own 2021
speeches is an obvious caveat, but the two speeches by then-Governor Phil Lowe also scored better
on these metrics than the sample from this year. It should be noted, though, that readability metrics do
not necessarily imply better clarity if the concepts discussed are more abstract.)
The ‘what’ has changed
As noted above, the content of RBA communication has evolved, too. As its intellectual framework has

begun to put more weight on the balance of supply and demand, and those invisible ‘star’ variables
(NAIRUs, neutral real rates, ‘financial conditions’, etc.), the communication to professional audiences
has increasingly focused on those topics.
More recently, the RBA has also placed more emphasis on the full range of transmission channels from
interest rates to inflation (though not, as we have noted previously, the labour supply channel). This is

understandable in a world of supply shocks. People need to understand that monetary policy is not
simply an exercise in hitting mortgage borrowers. We doubt they would be comforted, though, by the
Deputy Governor’s explanation this week that the ‘cashflow channel’ was the smallest of the
transmission channels from interest rates to inflation, with the exchange rate much more prominent.
That implies a lot of pain for a small group, for little effect on inflation. It raises the question of whether

other policy tools or institutional arrangements might get the same effect on inflation through the other
channels without hitting a minority of households disproportionately.
We have also seen a move away from using catchphrases to summarise the position. From ‘narrow
path’ to ‘not ruling anything in or out’ to ‘cautious and predictable’, once entrenched, a phrase can
outlast its usefulness.

### Page 4

Related articles
One thing that has not changed is the way the minutes record the presentations to the Board and the
members’ discussion. The minutes rarely convey much new information beyond the post-meeting
statements. Sometimes, though, the minutes reveal the Board’s reaction to the staff’s analysis. The
August 2024 minutes were a good example: the discussion highlighted just how sensitive the forecasts
were to key judgements by the staff.
The ‘why’ has not changed

Despite these changes, the underlying objectives of the RBA’s communication have not changed. It
wants to explain – and perhaps justify – its decisions. And it wants to describe how it interprets
incoming data, especially inflation, the labour market and the balance of supply and demand.
The RBA, like any central bank, is not really in the business of providing ‘forward guidance’ about
future moves. Circumstances change, so it can never guarantee a particular future path for interest

rates. Some academic literature argues that promising to keep rates low for a long time (or high, for
that matter) independently amplifies the transmission of monetary policy beyond the effect of current-
period interest rates alone, by shifting the short end of the yield curve. Central banks including the RBA
have certainly tried jawboning in this way from time to time. The prevailing view, though, is that the
potential cost of reputational loss if things do not turn out as predicted outweighs the benefits of a

slightly stronger transmission mechanism.
In this context, it is the interpretation of data that provides the signal about future policy directions. This
is why it was noteworthy that the Governor still characterised the labour market as ‘a little tight’ after the
September meeting, even though the Board’s post-meeting communication did not. We have been
noting for some time that the RBA staff have a downbeat view of supply capacity. Together with the

RBA’s oft-stated concerns about inflation, this is why the Deputy Governor’s ‘hawkishness’ did not
surprise us.

SpaceX 為全球 太空產業龍頭 ：SpaceX 業務主要分為 Connectivity 、AI
及 Space 三大部門。 Connectivity 以 Starlink 衛星連網服務為核心，涵

12% 。 Space X 在衛星通訊與火箭發射領域均具領先地位 ， 2025 年
SpaceX 共發射 165 次火箭， 全 球 火 箭 發 射共 325 次， 其 市 占 率 約
50.8% ，而在衛星通訊領域， Starlink 則為目前唯一可提供寬頻衛星通
訊服務給大規模用戶的供應商。  
圖表一：衛星產業市場規模

資料來源： SIA ，中信投顧整理  
Falcon 9 開啟火箭技術革命 ： 傳統 火箭多為一次性 設計 ，主要結構於
發 射後 拋棄， 使每 次發射 任務 均須重 新製 造與整 備， 導致 火 箭發 射成
本高昂。而 SpaceX 的獵鷹 9 號(Falcon 9 )於 2015 年首次完成一級火箭
回收，並於 2017 年實現回收助推器再次飛行，突破火箭一次性使用限
制，降低火箭發射成本， 使衛星寬頻通訊商業化可行性提高。 Falcon 9
每次發射衛星 報價 約在 3,200 美元 /公斤 ，SpaceX 自用成本更低 ，而最
新 的傳 統火 箭(Ariane 6) 發射 報價 則 約在 6,000 美元 /公斤 ， 相比 之 下
SpaceX 在火箭發射成本上有顯著優勢。 而 下 一 代 火 箭星艦 (Starship)
推測每公斤成本約在 600 美元 /公斤， 且目標 為全部回收並 將發射成本
進一步降低至 200 美元 /公斤 ，有望進一步帶動衛星發射數量 。 
圖表 二：各種類火箭比較

運載量 21.7噸 22.8噸 150噸

## Document 425: “易中天”首份三季报出炉！天孚通信单季营收同比增长31.5%，归母净利润增长55.3% _ 财报见闻

Source PDF: all report pdf/“易中天”首份三季报出炉！天孚通信单季营收同比增长31.5%，归母净利润增长55.3% _ 财报见闻.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-28
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

三季度，公司实现营业收⼊ 19.23 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 31.50% ；归⺟净利润 8.79 亿元，同⽐增
⻓ 55.30% ；拉⻓到前三季度，公司实现营业收⼊ 47.51 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 21.25% ；归⺟净利
润 20.83 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 42.18% 。
摘要：
AI 需求飙升的背景下，天孚通信交出了⼀份⾼增三季报。
2026 年第三季度，公司实现营业收⼊ 19.23 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 31.50% ；归⺟净利润 8.79 亿元，同
⽐增⻓ 55.30%；扣⾮归⺟净利润 8.79 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 55.78% 。
拉⻓到前三季度，公司实现营业收⼊ 47.51 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 21.25% ；归⺟净利润 20.83 亿元，同
⽐增⻓ 42.18%；扣⾮归⺟净利润 20.65 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 44.26% 。
财报给出的增⻓原因⾮常明确：全球⼈⼯智能⾏业加速发展、全球数据中⼼建设推进，带动⾼
速光器件产品需求持续稳定增⻓。换句话说， AI 算⼒产业链仍是天孚通信业绩增⻓的主线。
不过，利润⾼增背后也有⼏个需要关注的变化：应收账款和存货⼤幅上升，经营现⾦流增速低

于净利润增速；同时公司加⼤研发投⼊、战略性备货和设备预付款，显⽰其仍处于扩产和产品
迭代周期中。
单季度利润弹性突出，净利率继续抬升
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

第三季度，天孚通信收⼊ 19.23 亿元，同⽐增加 31.50% ；归⺟净利润 8.79 亿元，同⽐增加
55.30% 。
按单季度测算，公司第三季度归⺟净利率约 45.72% ，较上年同期约 38.71% 明显提升。扣⾮归⺟
净利润为 8.79 亿元，和归⺟净利润⼏乎持平，说明当季利润质量较⾼，并⾮依赖政府补助、投资
收益等⾮经常项⽬。
每股收益⽅⾯，第三季度基本每股收益 0.8062 元，同⽐增⻓ 54.98% ；稀释每股收益 0.8045 元，
同⽐增⻓ 55.04% 。加权平均净资产收益率为 12.91% ，同⽐提升 1.74 个百分点。
前三季度⽑利率⼤幅改善，成本端贡献明显
前三季度，公司营业收⼊ 47.51 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 21.25% ；营业成本 17.79 亿元，同⽐下降
5.69% 。
这组数据是财报中最值得关注的地⽅：收⼊增⻓的同时，营业成本反⽽下降，直接推升⽑利
率。按财报数据测算，前三季度公司⽑利约 29.72 亿元，⽑利率约 62.56% ；上年同期⽑利率约
51.87% ，同⽐提升超过 10 个百分点。

公司未在三季报中拆分具体产品⽑利变化，但从结果看，收⼊增⻓、产品结构、规模效应和成
本控制共同放⼤了利润弹性。前三季度营业利润 24.08 亿元，同⽐增⻓约 42.5% ；利润总额 24.04
亿元，同⽐增⻓约 42.0% ；归⺟净利润增速与营业利润增速基本匹配。
AI 和数据中⼼仍是核⼼驱动⼒
天孚通信在财报中明确表⽰，营业收⼊增⻓主要得益于全球⼈⼯智能⾏业加速发展与全球数据
中⼼建设，带动⾼速光器件产品需求持续稳定增⻓。
这意味着，公司当前业绩仍⾼度受益于 AI 算⼒基础设施投资周期。⾼速光器件作为数据中⼼内部
及数据中⼼之间⾼速互联的重要环节，在 AI 服务器、交换机、光模块升级中承担关键⻆⾊。
从前三季度表现看，天孚通信的增⻓并不只是收⼊端放量，更体现在盈利能⼒提升。扣⾮归⺟
净利润增速 44.26% ，⾼于收⼊增速 21.25% ，说明 AI 需求拉动下，公司经营杠杆正在体现。
研发费⽤⼤增近 48% ，汇兑损失拖累财务费⽤
费⽤端呈现 “ ⼀增⼀减 ” 的结构。
前三季度，公司研发费⽤ 2.96 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 47.71% ，主要由于研发项⽬投⼊及⼈⼒成本增

加。研发费⽤率约 6.22% ，较上年同期约 5.11% 有所提升。对于⾼速光通信器件企业⽽⾔， AI 数
据中⼼带来的产品速率升级和⼯艺要求提升，意味着研发投⼊仍是维持竞争⼒的关键。
销售费⽤为 1378.33 万元，同⽐下降 11.62% ；管理费⽤ 1.01 亿元，同⽐下降 5.25% 。销售和管理
费⽤保持克制，有助于利润释放。
压⼒主要来⾃财务费⽤。前三季度财务费⽤ 6174.50 万元，上年同期为 -3508.05 万元，同⽐增加
276.01% ，公司解释主要是汇兑损失同⽐增加。也就是说，汇率波动对利润形成了⼀定拖累。
此外，信⽤减值损失 4448.98 万元，同⽐增加 154.99% ，主要由于根据信⽤减值测试计提的坏账
准备增加。这与应收账款快速增⻓相互对应。

### Page 3

应收账款和存货⼤幅上升，现⾦转换弱于利润增⻓
截⾄三季度末，公司应收账款 19.45 亿元，较上年末增⻓ 73.33% ，公司称主要是销售增⻓带来的
⾃然增加。
存货增幅更⼤，期末存货 12.74 亿元，较上年末增⻓ 178.65% ，主要因为战略性备货带来的原材
料增加。预付款项 9218.70 万元，较上年末增⻓ 343.75% ，主要是预付材料款增加。
这说明公司⼀⽅⾯在承接需求增⻓，另⼀⽅⾯也在提前锁定原材料和产能资源。战略性备货通
常意味着企业对后续订单和交付节奏有信⼼，但也会带来营运资⾦占⽤，并对库存周转提出更
⾼要求。
经营现⾦流⽅⾯，前三季度经营活动产⽣的现⾦流量净额 14.62 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 8.75% ，增速低
于归⺟净利润 42.18% 的增速。经营现⾦流净额占归⺟净利润⽐例约 70% ，低于上年同期约 92%
的⽔平。
从收现看，前三季度销售商品、提供劳务收到现⾦ 44.33 亿元，同⽐增⻓约 20.6% ，与收⼊增速
基本匹配；但采购、职⼯薪酬、税费等现⾦流出也同步上升，尤其备货和扩张投⼊对现⾦流形

成占⽤。
资产负债表扩张，仍保持低杠杆
截⾄三季度末，天孚通信总资产 82.45 亿元，较上年末增⻓ 27.84% ；归属于上市公司股东的所有
者权益 65.80 亿元，较上年末增⻓ 19.50% 。
负债合计 16.59 亿元，资产负债率约 20.1% ，整体杠杆⽔平仍然较低。公司期末没有短期借款和
⻓期借款，应付票据 2.84 亿元、应付账款 7.07 亿元，分别较上年末增⻓ 207.20% 、 75.75% ，主
要与采购增加和承兑汇票⽀付⽅式增加有关。
合同负债 3.66 亿元，较上年末增⻓ 128.14% ，主要是预收客⼾款项增加。预收款项的增加，在⼀
定程度上也反映客⼾需求和订单执⾏的前置资⾦安排。
流动资产 62.09 亿元，流动负债 16.33 亿元，流动⽐率约 3.80 倍。公司货币资⾦ 26.59 亿元，虽较
上年末下降 11.26% ，但绝对规模仍较充裕。
投资现⾦流⼤幅流出，扩产与美元定存是主因
前三季度，公司投资活动产⽣的现⾦流量净额为 -21.61 亿元，上年同期为 -5044.78 万元。公司解

释，主要是报告期内购⼊美元定期存款增加影响。
现⾦流量表显⽰，前三季度购建固定资产、⽆形资产和其他⻓期资产⽀付现⾦ 4.91 亿元，反映产
能、设备及⼟地等投⼊仍在进⾏。期末其他⾮流动资产 1.52 亿元，较上年末增⻓ 446.60% ，主要
由于预付设备款增加；⽆形资产 1.16 亿元，较上年末增⻓ 28.75% ，主要是⼦公司购置⼟地。
此外，其他流动资产 5009.25 万元，较上年末增⻓ 134.38% ，公司称主要是报告期内已发⽣且应
资本化的港股上市费⽤。这意味着，公司推进港股上市相关⼯作已有费⽤体现。
现⾦及现⾦等价物⽅⾯，期末余额 9.39 亿元，较期初 28.64 亿元明显下降，除投资活动流出外，
还受到现⾦分红和汇率变动影响。前三季度汇率变动对现⾦及现⾦等价物影响为 -1.41 亿元。
⼤额分红落地，股权激励覆盖 740 ⼈

• 國防需求已由短期補庫存轉
為多年期擴產
《國防需求已由短期補庫存轉為多年期擴產》
美國主要航空與國防類股公布3Q26財報，中東衝突
再升溫導致國防需求高於商業航空 。以下是主要廠
商的分析 :
雷神∶受益於飛彈回補庫存 ，3Q26取得超過810億
美元商用與國防新訂單 ，其中國防訂單 590億美元 ，
包括約 230億美元 「戰斧 」飛彈訂單與 140億美元
「先進中程空對空飛彈 」（AMRAAM）訂單 ，帶
動總積壓訂單較上季增加 520億美元 ，累計訂單
3,400億美元 ，年增36%。在飛彈防空系統與商業
航空維修服務市場強勁帶動下 ，公司上調2026年營
收到973億美元、EPS 7.70美元(年增22%)。

洛克希德馬丁 ∶受益於飛彈與火控系統產量提升 ，
帶動3Q26營收與獲利優於預期。在PAC-3多年期合
約則推升下，積壓訂單增加616億美元達2,920億美
元。由於美國 、中東、北約國家飛彈補庫需求將持
續數年，所以公司上調2026年營收到810億美元(年
增8%)、EPS 30.55美元(年增42%)。
奇異航空 ∶受益於 LEAP交付 、備件 、維修及 shop
visit增加帶動 ，商用發動機及服務營收年增 21%，
3Q26營收優於預期。在商用航空備件、維修服務及
引擎交付需求優於預期 ，加上主要供應商材料投入
改善 ， 公司上調 2026 年營收到 508億美元 、EPS
7.75美元(年增26%)。
總結，雖然國防類股近期財報受益於產能提升維幅
上調全年財測 ，反觀油價高漲對於美國航空運輸業
造成全面性的衝擊，所以工業維持中立評等。

近期，受外部环境变化、市场风险偏好波动以及部分前期涨幅较大板块调整
等因素影响，A 股市场震荡有所加大，投资者信心受到一定影响。但从宏观
经济基本面、上市公司经营情况及资本市场政策环境来看，当前市场运行中
积极因素正在持续积累。我国经济增长保持韧性，部分行业景气度和企业盈
利有所改善，监管部门持续完善资本市场稳市机制，上市公司积极开展回购
增持，为稳定市场预期、增强投资者信心和推动资本市场中长期平稳健康发
展提供了重要支撑。 
一、近期宏观经济与资本市场形势 
近期宏观经济与资本市场总体呈现以下特征： 
一是宏观经济保持韧性，新动能持续壮大，为资本市场提供坚实的基本面支
撑。二是 A 股上市公司三季报亮点逐步显现，部分行业盈利改善增强市场基
本面支撑。三是市场估值呈现结构性分化，部分优质资产中长期投资价值进
一步显现。四是监管部门持续完善资本市场稳市机制，政策积极信号不断释
放。五是上市公司积极推进回购增持，主动传递发展信心，助力稳定市场预
期。 
二、后市展望 
展望后市，随着宏观政策持续落地显效、部分行业盈利改善趋势逐步明朗、
中长期资金入市机制进一步完善，以及资本市场改革不断深化，多重积极因
素有望形成合力，为市场信心持续修复和资本市场平稳健康发展提供更加坚
实的支撑。

近期，受外部环境变化、市场风险偏好波动以及部分前期涨幅较大板块调整等因素
影响，A 股市场震荡有所加大，投资者信心受到一定影响。但从宏观经济基本面、上市
公司经营情况及资本市场政策环境来看，当前市场运行中积极因素正在持续积累。我国
经济增长保持韧性，部分行业景气度和企业盈利有所改善，监管部门持续完善资本市场
稳市机制，上市公司积极开展回购增持，为稳定市场预期、增强投资者信心和推动资本
市场中长期平稳健康发展提供了重要支撑。
一、近期宏观经济与资本市场形势
一是宏观经济保持韧性，新动能持续壮大，为资本市场提供坚实的基本面支撑。国
家统计局数据显示，前三季度我国 GDP 同比增长 4.6%，其中三季度同比增长 4.5%，较
二季度回升 0.2 个百分点，经济运行呈现稳中有进、结构向优的积极态势。从产业结构
看，前三季度规模以上工业增加值同比增长5.3%，装备制造业和高技术制造业增加值分
别增长 10.2%和 14.3%，明显快于工业整体增速；服务业增加值同比增长 5.2%，对经济

增长的贡献率达到 66.3%。特别是人工智能、先进制造、数字经济等新兴领域保持较快
发展， 前三季度集成电路制造、 光电子器件制造行业增加值分别同比增长79.2%和 28.4%，
工业新动能对工业增长的贡献率超过五成，成为经济增长的重要支撑。与此同时，消费
品以旧换新、设备更新、地方政府专项债和新型政策性金融工具等政策持续落地，有望
进一步巩固经济回升基础。尽管当前消费、投资等内需指标仍然偏弱，但经济增长韧性
和产业转型升级的积极趋势值得关注，为资本市场中长期发展提供了重要支撑。
二是 A 股上市公司三季报亮点逐步显现，部分行业盈利改善增强市场基本面支撑。
随着三季报进入密集披露期，A 股上市公司业绩呈现结构性改善迹象。 据Wind 数据统计，
截至 10 月 23 日，A 股已有 174 家公司披露 2026 年三季报业绩预告，其中报喜（预增、
略增、扭亏、续盈）合计148 家，占比约 85%。在其中 86 家披露可比净利润同比增幅数
据的公司中，按预告净利润增幅中值计算，78 家公司预计净利润同比增长超过 100%，

占比超过九成。从行业分布看，AI 算力需求带动的半导体、光模块、PCB 等产业链保持
较高景气度，有色金属、基础化工等传统行业受益于价格回升和供需格局改善，部分企
业盈利明显修复。虽然目前披露样本尚不能代表 A 股整体业绩，行业间盈利分化仍较明
显，但新兴产业较快增长与部分传统产业盈利修复共同构成积极信号，反映出经济结构
调整中的积极变化正在向上市公司业绩逐步传导，有望为市场提供更坚实的盈利支撑。
三是市场估值呈现结构性分化，部分优质资产中长期投资价值进一步显现。经历近
期市场调整后，部分前期涨幅较大板块的估值压力有所释放，市场估值结构也出现新的
变化。从主要指数看，截至 10 月 23 日，上证指数滚动市盈率约 15 倍，处于近十年约
66%分位，整体处于历史估值区间的中等偏上水平；创业板指市盈率约31 倍，处于近十
年约 18%分位，属于历史相对偏低的估值区间，部分优质成长企业的中长期投资吸引力
有所提升； 科创50 指数市盈率约 110 倍， 处于指数发布以来约78%分位， 估值水平相对

偏高，反映出市场对半导体、人工智能等科技创新领域未来成长性的较高预期，其投资
价值仍需结合企业盈利兑现情况综合判断。整体来看，当前A 股不同板块估值分化较为
明显，部分基本面稳健、盈利能力较强、现金流良好的传统行业龙头，以及经过调整后
估值与成长性更加匹配的优质科技企业，值得从中长期视角持续关注。随着上市公司盈
利逐步改善、市场定价更加注重基本面，优质资产的投资价值有望进一步得到体现。
四是监管部门持续完善资本市场稳市机制，政策积极信号不断释放。近期监管部门
围绕维护资本市场平稳运行、增强市场内在稳定性持续作出部署。10 月 19 日，证监会
在 2026 金融街论坛年会上，提出坚持稳字当头，加强风险研判和政策工具储备， 巩固完
善稳市机制建设；推动健全适配长期投资的长周期考核机制，扩大中长期资金来源、渠
道和入市方式，持续深化公募基金改革，壮大市场稳定力量；同时鼓励有条件的上市公

### Page 4

宏观点评
请务必阅读最后特别声明与免责条款 www.ykzq.com  4 / 6
司加大回购增持力度。此外，证监会同意首批18 只主动 ETF 注册，按照单只产品 20 亿
元的募集上限测算，合计募集规模上限达 360 亿元，有望进一步丰富投资工具供给、吸
引增量资金入市。上述举措体现出监管部门维护资本市场稳定、优化投融资生态、提升
投资者回报的政策导向。随着相关改革措施逐步落地，资本市场长期资金基础和内在稳
定性有望进一步增强。
五是上市公司积极推进回购增持， 主动传递发展信心， 助力稳定市场预期。据 Wind
数据统计，2026 年 9 月 1 日至 10 月 23 日， 已有169 家 A 股上市公司新增披露回购预案，
拟回购金额上限合计约 244 亿元。从部分公司公告看，AI 算力产业链中的光模块等细分
领域企业较为积极，新易盛、中际旭创、天孚通信等公司近期相继披露回购相关安排，
体现出部分科技企业对自身经营前景和长期价值的积极判断。上市公司主动开展回购增
持、强化股东回报，有助于提振市场信心、改善投资者预期，推动形成上市公司质量提

升与投资者信心增强的良性循环。
总体来看，当前 A 股市场正处于宏观经济温和修复、产业结构加快转型、上市公司
盈利呈现结构性改善以及资本市场制度持续完善的阶段。必须看到，房地产市场调整、
国内有效需求不足、部分行业盈利承压以及外部环境不确定性等因素仍可能对市场形成
扰动，投资者对于短期波动和资产回撤的担忧具有现实基础。市场信心的持续改善，归
根到底仍有赖于经济基本面进一步巩固、上市公司盈利持续改善以及投资者获得感不断
增强。
二、后市展望
展望后市，随着宏观政策持续落地显效、部分行业盈利改善趋势逐步明朗、中长期
资金入市机制进一步完善，以及资本市场改革不断深化，多重积极因素有望形成合力，
为市场信心持续修复和资本市场平稳健康发展提供更加坚实的支撑。短期市场波动不改
变中国经济转型升级的长期趋势，也不改变优质企业的长期投资价值。投资者应理性看
待市场波动，更加关注企业基本面、盈利质量和中长期投资价值，对中国经济高质量发
展和资本市场长期发展保持信心。

### Page 5

宏观点评 
请务必阅读最后特别声明与免责条款 www.ykzq.com  5 / 6
